title:PvE Podcast 172 - The Best Pulse Rifles
description:Monument of Triumph new and reprised pulse rifles, discussed in the 23 June 2026 @PodvsEnemies podcast (https://open.spotify.com/episode/5VaQFYdKdyxabDbPHNNf1n)

// Blast Furnace - PvE Minor god 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling), (Tactical Mag, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Kinetic Tremors), (Chaos Reshaped)
//notes:Inspired by @Saint_Kabr and @ImpetusAlways (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing groups of Minor adds in activities rated Normal, Hard and Very Hard difficulty. Blast Furnace is back! It's still an Aggressive Burst pulse rifle which as a subfamily are currently rated D-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ as "essentially just a poor high-impact auto, but arguably worse due to 4-burst nature". The frame intrinsic offers built-in anti-Unstoppable and fires a four-round burst as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats make this the best in class by comparison with its peers - you can see the stats at https://destiny.report/w/2631356658/blast-furnace and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/2631356658 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil is vertical and a bit loose, so to tighten that up, look for a barrel perk that helps with Recoil Direction or, if you can handle it just go for extra handling, and then with the magazine perk go for something that helps improve magazine size or reload. For clearing groups of adds in general content, in the first trait perk column Kinetic Tremors emits three 6m AoE Kinetic shockwaves after 11 hits (10 when enhanced) within 3s of each other, which are treated as weapon damage so can inherit damage boosts from Chaos Reshaped in the second column, which stacks up to ×2 on dealing or recoving damage within a 5s window for at least 12s (11.5s when enhanced) - at ×1 it provides a ×1.2 damage boost for a refreshable 7s and then at ×2 provides ×1.35 damage for a refreshable 7s together with a healing burst of up to 35HP that also starts health regeneration. The Forge's Kin origin trait provides a ×1 stack of "Forge's Kin" for 5s after a kill, or on swapping from another Black Armory weapon kill within 7s. Each additional Black Armory weapon equipped adds an additional stack, each boosting the activation window by +2s together with increasing Reload Speed and Ammo Generation and damage boosts of ×1.05 and ×1.07 (×1.1 and ×1.13 enhanced). Alternatively, kills with the Indomitability origin trait provides 5% melee energy with a Darkness super equipped or 5% grenade energy with a Light super (6% for either enhanced). If you're interested, a blast furnace is an industrial smelter that relies on extreme, searing heat and a continuous blast of air to melt down raw iron ore into usable metal. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Blast Furnace - PvE Minor god AB 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling), (Tactical Mag, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Bewlidering Burst), (Firefly)
//notes:Inspired by @Saint_Kabr and @ImpetusAlways (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing groups of Minor adds in activities rated Normal difficulty. Blast Furnace is back! It's still an Aggressive Burst pulse rifle which as a subfamily are currently rated D-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ as "essentially just a poor high-impact auto, but arguably worse due to 4-burst nature". The frame intrinsic offers built-in anti-Unstoppable and fires a four-round burst as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats make this the best in class by comparison with its peers - you can see the stats at https://destiny.report/w/2631356658/blast-furnace and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/2631356658 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil is vertical and a bit loose, so to tighten that up, look for a barrel perk that helps with Recoil Direction or, if you can handle it just go for extra handling, and then with the magazine perk go for something that helps improve magazine size or reload. For clearing groups of adds in general content, in the first trait perk column Bewildering Burst procs for 6s (7s when enhanced) after finishing a reload following a kill, and then on the next hit launches a kinetic flashbang above the target that does damage and disorients enemies within 9m for 3.5s. In the second column, Firefly procs a 4m AoE Solar explosion on a crit kill plus +50 Reload Speed for 6s to help with proccing Bewildering Burst. The Forge's Kin origin trait provides a ×1 stack of "Forge's Kin" for 5s after a kill, or on swapping from another Black Armory weapon kill within 7s. Each additional Black Armory weapon equipped adds an additional stack, each boosting the activation window by +2s together with increasing Reload Speed and Ammo Generation and damage boosts of ×1.05 and ×1.07 (×1.1 and ×1.13 enhanced). Alternatively, kills with the Indomitability origin trait provides 5% melee energy with a Darkness super equipped or 5% grenade energy with a Light super (6% for either enhanced). If you're interested, a blast furnace is an industrial smelter that relies on extreme, searing heat and a continuous blast of air to melt down raw iron ore into usable metal. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Redrix's Estoc - PvE Minor god 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling), (Tactical Mag, Alloy Mag, Appended Mag, Flared Magwell), (Rimestealer, Crystalline Corpsebloom), (Headstone)
//notes:Inspired by @Saint_Kabr and @ImpetusAlways (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing groups of Minor adds in activities rated Normal difficulty, particularly with Stasis builds. The reprised Redrix's Estoc is a Legacy PR-55 frame pulse rifle which as a subfamily are currently rated D-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ as "essentially just a worse high-impact auto". The frame intrinsic offers built-in anti-Barrier and "dramatically increased accuracy, stability, and targeting while hip firing" as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats make this the best in class by comparison with its peers - you can see the stats at https://destiny.report/w/747743636/redrixs-estoc and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/747743636 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil is vertical but loose, so to tighten that up, look for a barrel perk that helps with Recoil Direction or, if you can handle it just go for extra handling, and then with the magazine perk go for something that helps improve magazine size or reload. For Stasis synergy and clearing groups of adds in general content, in the first trait perk column there's the choice of Rimestealer which generates ×1 stack (×2 stacks when enhanced) of Stasis Frost Armor on shattering a Stasis crystal or killing a frozen enemy, or Crystalline Corpsebloom which procs on a kill to provide a 4s buff - kills with the buff active generate a Stasis seeker which travels 3m before spawning a medium Stasis crystal (large when enhanced). In the second column, Headstone generates a Stasis crystal on a crit kill. The Stasis crystals are weapon-generated entities so can inherit damage buffs, and proc subclass fragments when shattered for AoE damage. You have a choice of stocks rather than origin traits - any can work so look for one that boosts your preferred weapon stat. If you're interested, Redrix-3 was an Exo Titan who was a legendary champion of the early Crucible, while an "estoc" is a 14th-century longsword designed to punch through chainmail or gaps in heavy plate armour. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Redrix's Estoc - PvE Minor god 2
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling), (Tactical Mag, Alloy Mag, Appended Mag, Flared Magwell), (Demolitionist, Firefly), (Headstone)
//notes:Inspired by @Saint_Kabr and @ImpetusAlways (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing groups of Minor adds in activities rated Normal difficulty, particularly with Stasis builds. The reprised Redrix's Estoc is a Legacy PR-55 frame pulse rifle which as a subfamily are currently rated D-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ as "essentially just a worse high-impact auto". The frame intrinsic offers built-in anti-Barrier and "dramatically increased accuracy, stability, and targeting while hip firing" as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats make this the best in class by comparison with its peers - you can see the stats at https://destiny.report/w/747743636/redrixs-estoc and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/747743636 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil is vertical but loose, so to tighten that up, look for a barrel perk that helps with Recoil Direction or, if you can handle it just go for extra handling, and then with the magazine perk go for something that helps improve magazine size or reload. For Stasis synergy and clearing groups of adds in general content, in the first trait perk column there's the choice of Demolitionist which generates chunk grenade ability energy on kills and an instant magazine refill on grenade use, for your neutral game or Firefly procs a 4m AoE Solar explosion on a crit kill plus +50 Reload Speed for 6s. In the second column, Headstone generates a Stasis crystal on a crit kill, these are weapon-generated entities so can inherit damage buffs, and proc weapon traits (like Demolitionist) and subclass fragments when shattered for AoE damage. Firefly and Headstone together will create and shatter the Stasis crystal with a single crit kill. You have a choice of stocks rather than origin traits - any can work so look for one that boosts your preferred weapon stat. If you're interested, Redrix-3 was an Exo Titan who was a legendary champion of the early Crucible, while an "estoc" is a 14th-century longsword designed to punch through chainmail or gaps in heavy plate armour. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Redrix's Estoc - PvE Minor god 3
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling), (Tactical Mag, Alloy Mag, Appended Mag, Flared Magwell), (Demolitionist, Crystalline Corpsebloom), (Frenzy)
//notes:Inspired by @Saint_Kabr and @ImpetusAlways (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for use against Minor adds in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. The reprised Redrix's Estoc is a Legacy PR-55 frame pulse rifle which as a subfamily are currently rated D-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ as "essentially just a worse high-impact auto". The frame intrinsic offers built-in anti-Barrier and "dramatically increased accuracy, stability, and targeting while hip firing" as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats make this the best in class by comparison with its peers - you can see the stats at https://destiny.report/w/747743636/redrixs-estoc and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/747743636 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil is vertical but loose, so to tighten that up, look for a barrel perk that helps with Recoil Direction or, if you can handle it just go for extra handling, and then with the magazine perk go for something that helps improve magazine size or reload. For add-clearing, in the first trait perk column there's the choice of Demolitionist which generates chunk grenade ability energy on kills and an instant magazine refill on grenade use, for your neutral game or Crystalline Corpsebloom which procs on a kill to provide a 4s buff - kills with the buff active generate a Stasis seeker which travels 3m before spawning a medium Stasis crystal (large when enhanced) - these are weapon-generated entities so can inherit damage buffs, and proc Stasis subclass fragments when shattered for AoE damage. In the second column Frenzy, after being in combat for 12s - that's dealing or taking damage - boosts Handling and Reload Speed by +100 and damage by ×1.15 for a refreshable 7.2s. You have a choice of stocks rather than origin traits - any can work so look for one that boosts your preferred weapon stat. If you're interested, Redrix-3 was an Exo Titan who was a legendary champion of the early Crucible, while an "estoc" is a 14th-century longsword designed to punch through chainmail or gaps in heavy plate armour. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Redrix's Estoc - PvE Minor god 4
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling), (Tactical Mag, Alloy Mag, Appended Mag, Flared Magwell), (Firefly, Demolitionist, Crystalline Corpsebloom), (Elemental Honing)
//notes:Inspired by @Saint_Kabr and @ImpetusAlways (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for use against Minor adds in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty, particularly with Prismatic builds. The reprised Redrix's Estoc is a Legacy PR-55 frame pulse rifle which as a subfamily are currently rated D-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ as "essentially just a worse high-impact auto". The frame intrinsic offers built-in anti-Barrier and "dramatically increased accuracy, stability, and targeting while hip firing" as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats make this the best in class by comparison with its peers - you can see the stats at https://destiny.report/w/747743636/redrixs-estoc and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/747743636 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil is vertical but loose, so to tighten that up, look for a barrel perk that helps with Recoil Direction or, if you can handle it just go for extra handling, and then with the magazine perk go for something that helps improve magazine size or reload. For add-clearing, in the first trait perk column there's the choice of Firefly which procs a 4m AoE Solar explosion on a crit kill plus +50 Reload Speed for 6s, Demolitionist which generates chunk grenade ability energy on kills and an instant magazine refill on grenade use, for your neutral game, or Crystalline Corpsebloom which procs on a kill to provide a 4s buff - kills with the buff active generate a Stasis seeker which travels 3m before spawning a medium Stasis crystal (large when enhanced) - these are weapon-generated entities so can inherit damage buffs, and proc Stasis subclass fragments when shattered for AoE damage. In the second column, syergising well with Prismatic builds, Elemental Honing grants an escalating stack of damage up to ×1.35 for 20s (23s when enhanced) for each elemental damage type inflicted, which can be refreshed with a new damage type until you've run through all 5. Handily, crit kills with Firefly that inflict solar damage alongside the stasis damage from the weapon will grant an instant ×2 stack to get you started. You have a choice of stocks rather than origin traits - any can work so look for one that boosts your preferred weapon stat. If you're interested, Redrix-3 was an Exo Titan who was a legendary champion of the early Crucible, while an "estoc" is a 14th-century longsword designed to punch through chainmail or gaps in heavy plate armour. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Chattering Bone - PvE Minor god 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling), (Tactical Mag, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Attrition Orbs), (Kinetic Tremors)
//notes:Inspired by @Saint_Kabr and @ImpetusAlways (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing groups of Minor adds in activities rated Normal, Hard and Very Hard difficulty. The reprised Chattering Bone is a Lightweight pulse rifle which as a subfamily are currently rated D-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ as "essentially just a worse high-impact auto". The frame intrinsic offers built-in anti-Overload, and +20 to the hidden Mobility stat and ×1.0625 base movement speed as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are decent by comparison with its peers - you can see the stats at https://destiny.report/w/830651379/chattering-bone and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/830651379 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil is right and loose, so to tighten that up, look for a barrel perk that helps with Recoil Direction or, if you can handle it just go for extra handling, and then with the magazine perk go for something that helps improve magazine size or reload. For clearing groups of adds, in the first trait perk column Attrition Orbs spawns a power orb after ~22 hits within 5s of each other to proc armour mods. In the second column, to pair with that, Kinetic Tremors emits three 6m AoE Kinetic shockwaves after ~11 hits (~10 when enhanced) within 3s of each other, which are treated as weapon damage so inherit damage boosts and also proc weapon effects like Attrition Orbs' orb generation. The Elliptical Orbit origin trait passively grants Inner Orbit, boosting Stability, Reload Speed and Flinch Resist and, after sprinting for 1.5s procs Outer Orbit for 3s to boost Ammo Generation. Alternatively, Explosive Pact grants stacks up to ×5 on grenade use and grenade kills (or healing orb pickups for healing grenades) to boost Stability, Reload Speed and Reload Animation Scalar for 7.5s. If you're interested, the name refers to the eerie, whispering voice heard by the bearers of a dead Ahamkara's bones, tricking them into making deals. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Chattering Bone - PvE Minor god 2
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling), (Tactical Mag, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Bewildering Burst), (Chaos Reshaped, Kinetic Tremors, One For All)
//notes:Inspired by @Saint_Kabr and @ImpetusAlways (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing groups of Minor adds in activities rated Normal, Hard and Very Hard difficulty. The reprised Chattering Bone is a Lightweight pulse rifle which as a subfamily are currently rated D-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ as "essentially just a worse high-impact auto". The frame intrinsic offers built-in anti-Overload, and +20 to the hidden Mobility stat and ×1.0625 base movement speed as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are decent by comparison with its peers - you can see the stats at https://destiny.report/w/830651379/chattering-bone and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/830651379 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil is right and loose, so to tighten that up, look for a barrel perk that helps with Recoil Direction or, if you can handle it just go for extra handling, and then with the magazine perk go for something that helps improve magazine size or reload. For clearing groups of adds, in the first trait perk column Bewildering Burst procs for 6s (7s when enhanced) after finishing a reload following a kill, and then on the next hit launches a kinetic flashbang above the target that does damage and disorients enemies within 9m for 3.5s. In the second column, to pair with that, and clean up the stunned enemies, Chaos Reshaped stacks up to ×2 on dealing or receiving damage within a 5s window for at least 12s (11.5s when enhanced) - at ×1 it provides a ×1.2 damage boost for a refreshable 7s and then at ×2 provides ×1.35 damage for a refreshable 7s together with a healing burst of up to 35HP that also starts health regeneration, Kinetic Tremors emits three 6m AoE Kinetic shockwaves after 11 hits (10 when enhanced) within 3s of each other, which are treated as weapon damage so inherit damage boosts, or One For All boosts damage by ×1.35 for a non-refreshable 10s (11s when enhanced) after hitting three separate targets within 3s of each. The Elliptical Orbit origin trait passively grants Inner Orbit, boosting Stability, Reload Speed and Flinch Resist and, after sprinting for 1.5s procs Outer Orbit for 3s to boost Ammo Generation. Alternatively, Explosive Pact grants stacks up to ×5 on grenade use and grenade kills (or healing orb pickups for healing grenades) to boost Stability, Reload Speed and Reload Animation Scalar for 7.5s. If you're interested, the name refers to the eerie, whispering voice heard by the bearers of a dead Ahamkara's bones, tricking them into making deals. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

//notes:Inspired by @Saint_Kabr and @ImpetusAlways (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal, Hard and Very Hard difficulty. The reprised Chattering Bone is a Lightweight pulse rifle which as a subfamily are currently rated D-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ as "essentially just a worse high-impact auto". The frame intrinsic offers built-in anti-Overload, and +20 to the hidden Mobility stat and ×1.0625 base movement speed as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are decent by comparison with its peers - you can see the stats at https://destiny.report/w/830651379/chattering-bone and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/830651379 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil is right and loose, so to tighten that up, look for a barrel perk that helps with Recoil Direction or, if you can handle it just go for extra handling, and then with the magazine perk go for something that helps improve magazine size or reload. For tougher adds you can roll double damage trait perks. In the first column Kill Clip boosts damage by ×1.2 for 5s (5.5s when enhanced) when reloading within 3.7s of a kill. In the second column, to combine with that, Chaos Reshaped stacks up to ×2 on dealing or receiving damage within a 5s window for at least 12s (11.5s enhanced) - at ×1 it provides a ×1.2 damage boost for a refreshable 7s and then at ×2 provides ×1.35 damage for a refreshable 7s together with a healing burst of up to 35HP that also starts health regeneration, Kinetic Tremors emits three 6m AoE Kinetic shockwaves after 11 hits (10 enhanced) within 3s of each other, which are treated as weapon damage so inherit Kill Clip's damage boost, or One For All boosts damage by ×1.35 for a non-refreshable 10s (11s enhanced) after hitting three separate targets within 3s of each. The Elliptical Orbit origin trait passively grants Inner Orbit, boosting Stability, Reload Speed and Flinch Resist and, after sprinting for 1.5s procs Outer Orbit for 3s to boost Ammo Generation. Alternatively, Explosive Pact grants stacks up to ×5 on grenade use and grenade kills (or healing orb pickups for healing grenades) to boost Stability, Reload Speed and Reload Animation Scalar for 7.5s. If you're interested, the name refers to the eerie, whispering voice heard by the bearers of a dead Ahamkara's bones, tricking them into making deals. The reprised Chattering Bone is a Lightweight pulse rifle which as a subfamily are currently rated D-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ as "essentially just a worse high-impact auto". // Chattering Bone - PvE Minor god 2
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling), (Tactical Mag, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Bewildering Burst), (Chaos Reshaped, Kinetic Tremors, One For All)
//notes:Inspired by @Saint_Kabr and @ImpetusAlways (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing groups of Minor adds in activities rated Normal, Hard and Very Hard difficulty. The reprised Chattering Bone is a Lightweight pulse rifle which as a subfamily are currently rated D-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ as "essentially just a worse high-impact auto". The frame intrinsic offers built-in anti-Overload, and +20 to the hidden Mobility stat and ×1.0625 base movement speed as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are decent by comparison with its peers - you can see the stats at https://destiny.report/w/4157371152/chattering-bone and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/4157371152 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil is right and loose, so to tighten that up, look for a barrel perk that helps with Recoil Direction or, if you can handle it just go for extra handling, and then with the magazine perk go for something that helps improve magazine size or reload. For clearing groups of adds, in the first trait perk column Bewildering Burst procs for 6s (7s when enhanced) after finishing a reload following a kill, and then on the next hit launches a kinetic flashbang above the target that does damage and disorients enemies within 9m for 3.5s. In the second column, to pair with that, and clean up the stunned enemies, Chaos Reshaped stacks up to ×2 on dealing or receiving damage within a 5s window for at least 12s (11.5s when enhanced) - at ×1 it provides a ×1.2 damage boost for a refreshable 7s and then at ×2 provides ×1.35 damage for a refreshable 7s together with a healing burst of up to 35HP that also starts health regeneration, Kinetic Tremors emits three 6m AoE Kinetic shockwaves after 11 hits (10 when enhanced) within 3s of each other, which are treated as weapon damage so inherit damage boosts, or One For All boosts damage by ×1.35 for a non-refreshable 10s (11s when enhanced) after hitting three separate targets within 3s of each. The Elliptical Orbit origin trait passively grants Inner Orbit, boosting Stability, Reload Speed and Flinch Resist and, after sprinting for 1.5s procs Outer Orbit for 3s to boost Ammo Generation. Alternatively, Explosive Pact grants stacks up to ×5 on grenade use and grenade kills (or healing orb pickups for healing grenades) to boost Stability, Reload Speed and Reload Animation Scalar for 7.5s. If you're interested, the name refers to the eerie, whispering voice heard by the bearers of a dead Ahamkara's bones, tricking them into making deals. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

The frame intrinsic offers built-in anti-Overload, and +20 to the hidden Mobility stat and ×1.0625 base movement speed as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are decent by comparison with its peers - you can see the stats at https://destiny.report/w/4157371152/chattering-bone and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/4157371152 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil is right and loose, so to tighten that up, look for a barrel perk that helps with Recoil Direction or, if you can handle it just go for extra handling, and then with the magazine perk go for something that helps improve magazine size or reload. For tougher adds you can roll double damage trait perks. In the first column Kill Clip boosts damage by ×1.2 for 5s (5.5s when enhanced) when reloading within 3.7s of a kill. In the second column, to combine with that, Chaos Reshaped stacks up to ×2 on dealing or receiving damage within a 5s window for at least 12s (11.5s enhanced) - at ×1 it provides a ×1.2 damage boost for a refreshable 7s and then at ×2 provides ×1.35 damage for a refreshable 7s together with a healing burst of up to 35HP that also starts health regeneration, Kinetic Tremors emits three 6m AoE Kinetic shockwaves after 11 hits (10 enhanced) within 3s of each other, which are treated as weapon damage so inherit Kill Clip's damage boost, or One For All boosts damage by ×1.35 for a non-refreshable 10s (11s enhanced) after hitting three separate targets within 3s of each. The Elliptical Orbit origin trait passively grants Inner Orbit, boosting Stability, Reload Speed and Flinch Resist and, after sprinting for 1.5s procs Outer Orbit for 3s to boost Ammo Generation. Alternatively, Explosive Pact grants stacks up to ×5 on grenade use and grenade kills (or healing orb pickups for healing grenades) to boost Stability, Reload Speed and Reload Animation Scalar for 7.5s. If you're interested, the name refers to the eerie, whispering voice heard by the bearers of a dead Ahamkara's bones, tricking them into making deals. // Nullify - PvE Minor god 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling), (Tactical Mag, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Collective Demolition), (Desperate Measures, Adrenaline Junkie)
//notes:Inspired by @Saint_Kabr and @ImpetusAlways (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal difficulty, particularly with Solar grenade builds. The updated Nullify is a Heavy Burst pulse rifle which as a subfamily are currently rated D-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ as "essentially just a poor high-impact auto, but arguably worse due to 4-burst nature". The frame intrinsic offers built-in anti-Unstoppable, fires a 2-round burst and grants ×0.9 flinch when ADS as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are pretty much best in class by comparison with its peers - you can see the stats at https://destiny.report/w/859869931/nullify and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/859869931 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil is left and a little loose, so to tighten that up, look for a barrel perk that helps with Recoil Direction or, if you can handle it, just go for extra Handling, and then with the magazine perk go for something that helps improve magazine size or reload. The perk pool is stacked, if a little on the large size, with two new trait perks in each column for Moment of Triumph. Of the new ones, Collective Demolition provides grenade ability energy on hits after interacting with an elemental pickup - Firesprites and Void Breaches for 8s, Ionic Traces, Stasis Shards for 4s and Tangles and Arc Elemental Orbs 4s each for pickup and explosion when thrown. You can use the additional grenades to proc damage from Desperate Measures, which provides up to ×3 stacks for max ×1.3 damage for a refreshable 7s (7.5s enhanced) for a weapon and then two ability kills, or Adrenaline Junkie which generates up to ×5 stacks on kills for +20 handling and a max ×1.33 damage, with a grenade kill giving an instant 5 stacks, for a refreshable 4.5s (5s when enhanced). The Collective Purpose origin trait provides stacks on fireteam members within 15m up to ×5 which boost Range, Handling and a Draw Time scalar per stack. If you're interested, "nullify" means to invalidate, cancel out, or effectively reduce something to nothing. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Nullify - PvE Minor god 2
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling), (Tactical Mag, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Demolitionist), (Meganeura)
//notes:Inspired by @Saint_Kabr and @ImpetusAlways (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal difficulty, particularly with Solar grenade builds. The updated Nullify is a Heavy Burst pulse rifle which as a subfamily are currently rated D-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ as "essentially just a poor high-impact auto, but arguably worse due to 4-burst nature". The frame intrinsic offers built-in anti-Unstoppable, fires a 2-round burst and grants ×0.9 flinch when ADS as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are pretty much best in class by comparison with its peers - you can see the stats at https://destiny.report/w/859869931/nullify and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/859869931 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil is left and a little loose, so to tighten that up, look for a barrel perk that helps with Recoil Direction or, if you can handle it, just go for extra Handling, and then with the magazine perk go for something that helps improve magazine size or reload. The perk pool is stacked, if a little on the large size, with two new trait perks in each column for Moment of Triumph. Of the new ones, you could pair Demolitionist to generate chunk grenade ability energy on kills and an instant magazine refill on grenade use with Meganeura which builds up to ×3 stacks on crit hits for a refreshable 3.5s (4s when enhanced) for AoE solar explosive damage in a 7.5m radius on a crit kill - the explosion is treated as weapon damage so inherits damage boosts and also procs weapon effects like Demolitionist. The Collective Purpose origin trait provides stacks on fireteam members within 15m up to ×5 which boost Range, Handling and a Draw Time scalar per stack. If you're interested, "nullify" means to invalidate, cancel out, or effectively reduce something to nothing. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Nullify (Adept) - PvE Minor god 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling), (Tactical Mag, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Collective Demolition), (Desperate Measures, Adrenaline Junkie)
//notes:Inspired by @Saint_Kabr and @ImpetusAlways (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal difficulty, particularly with Solar grenade builds. The updated Nullify is a Heavy Burst pulse rifle which as a subfamily are currently rated D-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ as "essentially just a poor high-impact auto, but arguably worse due to 4-burst nature". The frame intrinsic offers built-in anti-Unstoppable, fires a 2-round burst and grants ×0.9 flinch when ADS as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are pretty much best in class by comparison with its peers - you can see the stats at https://destiny.report/w/892183998/nullify and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/892183998 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil is left and a little loose, so to tighten that up, look for a barrel perk that helps with Recoil Direction or, if you can handle it, just go for extra Handling, and then with the magazine perk go for something that helps improve magazine size or reload. The perk pool is stacked, if a little on the large size, with two new trait perks in each column for Moment of Triumph. Of the new ones, Collective Demolition provides grenade ability energy on hits after interacting with an elemental pickup - Firesprites and Void Breaches for 8s, Ionic Traces, Stasis Shards for 4s and Tangles and Arc Elemental Orbs 4s each for pickup and explosion when thrown. You can use the additional grenades to proc damage from Desperate Measures, which provides up to ×3 stacks for max ×1.3 damage for a refreshable 7s (7.5s enhanced) for a weapon and then two ability kills, or Adrenaline Junkie which generates up to ×5 stacks on kills for +20 handling and a max ×1.33 damage, with a grenade kill giving an instant 5 stacks, for a refreshable 4.5s (5s when enhanced). The Collective Purpose origin trait provides stacks on fireteam members within 15m up to ×5 which boost Range, Handling and a Draw Time scalar per stack. If you're interested, "nullify" means to invalidate, cancel out, or effectively reduce something to nothing. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Oversoul Edict - PvE Minor god 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling), (High-Caliber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Supercharged Magazine, Eddy Current), (Jolting Feedback)
//notes:Inspired by @Saint_Kabr and @ImpetusAlways (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal, Hard and Very Hard difficulty, particularly with Arc subclasses. The updated Oversoul Edict is a Lightweight pulse rifle which as a subfamily are currently rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ as "essentially just a worse high-impact auto". The frame intrinsic offers built-in anti-Overload, and +20 to the hidden Mobility stat and ×1.0625 base movement speed as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are decent by comparison with its peers - you can see the stats at https://destiny.report/w/1098171824/oversoul-edictand hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/1098171824 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil is left and quite loose, so to tighten that up, look for a barrel perk that helps with Recoil Direction or, if you can handle it just go for extra handling, and then with the magazine perk any of the combat effect mags works or go for something that helps improve magazine size or reload. For single target damage, synergising with arc builds, in the first trait perk column, Supercharged Magazine refills 5% of the magazine every 1.5s while you're Amplified or, after you sprint when Amplified to gain Speed Booster this accelerates to 5% every 0.25s while the buff is active, overflowing the magazine to up to ×2 capacity, or Eddy Current boosts Handling with a ×0.95 animation scalar and +20 Reload Speed with a ×0.95 animation scalar for 3s after sprinting for 1.5s, which is all applied instantly with +60 to Reload Speed when Amplified. For damage, Jolting Feedback applies Arc Jolt after 14 hits (13 when enhanced and 9 when amplified) to tougher enemies who can survive the weapon damage, triggering chain lightning on further hits for both target and AoE damage. The Cursed Thrall origin trait synergises well with melee builds - killing an enemy with a melee will proc a 4.5m radius Cursed Thrall AoE explosion for 7.5s (8.5s enhanced) on the next weapon kill. If you're interested, an edict is an official order or decree issued by an authority. So the name implies that the weapon acts as a manifested extension of the Hive Oversoul's power, enforcing the absolute, violent decree of the Hive gods. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Oversoul Edict - PvE Minor god 2
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling), (High-Caliber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Rolling Storm, Supercharged Magazine, Eddy Current), (Voltshot)
//notes:Inspired by @Saint_Kabr and @ImpetusAlways (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing add groups in activities rated Normal difficulty, particularly with Arc subclasses. The updated Oversoul Edict is a Lightweight pulse rifle which as a subfamily are currently rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ as "essentially just a worse high-impact auto". The frame intrinsic offers built-in anti-Overload, and +20 to the hidden Mobility stat and ×1.0625 base movement speed as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are decent by comparison with its peers - you can see the stats at https://destiny.report/w/1098171824/oversoul-edictand hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/1098171824 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil is left and quite loose, so to tighten that up, look for a barrel perk that helps with Recoil Direction or, if you can handle it just go for extra handling, and then with the magazine perk any of the combat effect mags works or go for something that helps improve magazine size or reload. For groups of adds, synergising with arc builds, in the first trait perk column for double damage perks, Rolling Storm generates ×1 stack of Arc Bolt Charge (×2 when Amplified) on a kill, to trigger a lightning bolt at ×10 stacks for both target and AoE damage. Alternatively, Supercharged Magazine refills 5% of the magazine every 1.5s while you're Amplified or, after you sprint when Amplified to gain Speed Booster this accelerates to 5% every 0.25s while the buff is active, overflowing the magazine to up to ×2 capacity, or Eddy Current boosts Handling with a ×0.95 animation scalar and +20 Reload Speed with a ×0.95 animation scalar for 3s after sprinting for 1.5s, which is all applied instantly with +60 to Reload Speed when Amplified. In the second column, Voltshot applies Arc Jolt to the next target you hit within 7s (8s when enhanced), after a reload within 5.3s of a kill, for additional damage to the target on further hits plus AoE chain lightning damage on nearby enemies. The Cursed Thrall origin trait synergises well with melee builds - killing an enemy with a melee will proc a 4.5m radius Cursed Thrall AoE explosion for 7.5s (8.5s enhanced) on the next weapon kill. If you're interested, an edict is an official order or decree issued by an authority. So the name implies that the weapon acts as a manifested extension of the Hive Oversoul's power, enforcing the absolute, violent decree of the Hive gods. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Oversoul Edict - PvE Minor god 3
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling), (High-Caliber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Rolling Storm, Supercharged Magazine, Eddy Current, Demolitionist), (Gear Shift)
//notes:Inspired by @Saint_Kabr and @ImpetusAlways (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing add groups in activities rated Normal difficulty, particularly with Arc subclasses. The updated Oversoul Edict is a Lightweight pulse rifle which as a subfamily are currently rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ as "essentially just a worse high-impact auto". The frame intrinsic offers built-in anti-Overload, and +20 to the hidden Mobility stat and ×1.0625 base movement speed as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are decent by comparison with its peers - you can see the stats at https://destiny.report/w/1098171824/oversoul-edictand hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/1098171824 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil is left and quite loose, so to tighten that up, look for a barrel perk that helps with Recoil Direction or, if you can handle it just go for extra handling, and then with the magazine perk any of the combat effect mags works or go for something that helps improve magazine size or reload. For groups of adds, synergising with arc builds, in the first trait perk column for double damage perks, Rolling Storm generates ×1 stack of Arc Bolt Charge (×2 when Amplified) on a kill, to trigger a lightning bolt at ×10 stacks for both target and AoE damage. Alternatively, Supercharged Magazine refills 5% of the magazine every 1.5s while you're Amplified or, after you sprint when Amplified to gain Speed Booster this accelerates to 5% every 0.25s while the buff is active, overflowing the magazine to up to ×2 capacity, or Eddy Current boosts Handling with a ×0.95 animation scalar and +20 Reload Speed with a ×0.95 animation scalar for 3s after sprinting for 1.5s, which is all applied instantly with +60 to Reload Speed when Amplified. There's also Demolitionist to generate chunk grenade ability energy on kills and an instant magazine refill on grenade use. In the second column, Gear Shift provides up to ×5 stacks for 15s on reloads following a weapon kill or becoming Amplified, providing up to max ×1.35 damage. The Cursed Thrall origin trait synergises well with melee builds - killing an enemy with a melee will proc a 4.5m radius Cursed Thrall AoE explosion for 7.5s (8.5s enhanced) on the next weapon kill. If you're interested, an edict is an official order or decree issued by an authority. So the name implies that the weapon acts as a manifested extension of the Hive Oversoul's power, enforcing the absolute, violent decree of the Hive gods. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Oversoul Edict (Adept) - PvE Minor god 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling), (High-Caliber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Supercharged Magazine, Eddy Current), (Jolting Feedback)
//notes:Inspired by @Saint_Kabr and @ImpetusAlways (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal, Hard and Very Hard difficulty, particularly with Arc subclasses. The updated Oversoul Edict is a Lightweight pulse rifle which as a subfamily are currently rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ as "essentially just a worse high-impact auto". The frame intrinsic offers built-in anti-Overload, and +20 to the hidden Mobility stat and ×1.0625 base movement speed as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are decent by comparison with its peers - you can see the stats at https://destiny.report/w/578105049/oversoul-edictand hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/578105049 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil is left and quite loose, so to tighten that up, look for a barrel perk that helps with Recoil Direction or, if you can handle it just go for extra handling, and then with the magazine perk any of the combat effect mags works or go for something that helps improve magazine size or reload. For single target damage, synergising with arc builds, in the first trait perk column, Supercharged Magazine refills 5% of the magazine every 1.5s while you're Amplified or, after you sprint when Amplified to gain Speed Booster this accelerates to 5% every 0.25s while the buff is active, overflowing the magazine to up to ×2 capacity, or Eddy Current boosts Handling with a ×0.95 animation scalar and +20 Reload Speed with a ×0.95 animation scalar for 3s after sprinting for 1.5s, which is all applied instantly with +60 to Reload Speed when Amplified. For damage, Jolting Feedback applies Arc Jolt after 14 hits (13 when enhanced and 9 when amplified) to tougher enemies who can survive the weapon damage, triggering chain lightning on further hits for both target and AoE damage. The Cursed Thrall origin trait synergises well with melee builds - killing an enemy with a melee will proc a 4.5m radius Cursed Thrall AoE explosion for 7.5s (8.5s enhanced) on the next weapon kill. If you're interested, an edict is an official order or decree issued by an authority. So the name implies that the weapon acts as a manifested extension of the Hive Oversoul's power, enforcing the absolute, violent decree of the Hive gods. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Oversoul Edict (Adept) - PvE Minor god 2
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling), (High-Caliber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Rolling Storm, Supercharged Magazine, Eddy Current), (Voltshot)
//notes:Inspired by @Saint_Kabr and @ImpetusAlways (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing add groups in activities rated Normal difficulty, particularly with Arc subclasses. The updated Oversoul Edict is a Lightweight pulse rifle which as a subfamily are currently rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ as "essentially just a worse high-impact auto". The frame intrinsic offers built-in anti-Overload, and +20 to the hidden Mobility stat and ×1.0625 base movement speed as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are decent by comparison with its peers - you can see the stats at https://destiny.report/w/578105049/oversoul-edictand hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/578105049 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil is left and quite loose, so to tighten that up, look for a barrel perk that helps with Recoil Direction or, if you can handle it just go for extra handling, and then with the magazine perk any of the combat effect mags works or go for something that helps improve magazine size or reload. For groups of adds, synergising with arc builds, in the first trait perk column for double damage perks, Rolling Storm generates ×1 stack of Arc Bolt Charge (×2 when Amplified) on a kill, to trigger a lightning bolt at ×10 stacks for both target and AoE damage. Alternatively, Supercharged Magazine refills 5% of the magazine every 1.5s while you're Amplified or, after you sprint when Amplified to gain Speed Booster this accelerates to 5% every 0.25s while the buff is active, overflowing the magazine to up to ×2 capacity, or Eddy Current boosts Handling with a ×0.95 animation scalar and +20 Reload Speed with a ×0.95 animation scalar for 3s after sprinting for 1.5s, which is all applied instantly with +60 to Reload Speed when Amplified. In the second column, Voltshot applies Arc Jolt to the next target you hit within 7s (8s when enhanced), after a reload within 5.3s of a kill, for additional damage to the target on further hits plus AoE chain lightning damage on nearby enemies. The Cursed Thrall origin trait synergises well with melee builds - killing an enemy with a melee will proc a 4.5m radius Cursed Thrall AoE explosion for 7.5s (8.5s enhanced) on the next weapon kill. If you're interested, an edict is an official order or decree issued by an authority. So the name implies that the weapon acts as a manifested extension of the Hive Oversoul's power, enforcing the absolute, violent decree of the Hive gods. // Oversoul Edict (Adept) - PvE Minor god 3
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling), (High-Caliber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Rolling Storm, Supercharged Magazine, Eddy Current, Demolitionist), (Gear Shift)
//notes:Inspired by @Saint_Kabr and @ImpetusAlways (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing add groups in activities rated Normal difficulty, particularly with Arc subclasses. The updated Oversoul Edict is a Lightweight pulse rifle which as a subfamily are currently rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ as "essentially just a worse high-impact auto". The frame intrinsic offers built-in anti-Overload, and +20 to the hidden Mobility stat and ×1.0625 base movement speed as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are decent by comparison with its peers - you can see the stats at https://destiny.report/w/578105049/oversoul-edictand hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/578105049 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil is left and quite loose, so to tighten that up, look for a barrel perk that helps with Recoil Direction or, if you can handle it just go for extra handling, and then with the magazine perk any of the combat effect mags works or go for something that helps improve magazine size or reload. For groups of adds, synergising with arc builds, in the first trait perk column for double damage perks, Rolling Storm generates ×1 stack of Arc Bolt Charge (×2 when Amplified) on a kill, to trigger a lightning bolt at ×10 stacks for both target and AoE damage. Alternatively, Supercharged Magazine refills 5% of the magazine every 1.5s while you're Amplified or, after you sprint when Amplified to gain Speed Booster this accelerates to 5% every 0.25s while the buff is active, overflowing the magazine to up to ×2 capacity, or Eddy Current boosts Handling with a ×0.95 animation scalar and +20 Reload Speed with a ×0.95 animation scalar for 3s after sprinting for 1.5s, which is all applied instantly with +60 to Reload Speed when Amplified. There's also Demolitionist to generate chunk grenade ability energy on kills and an instant magazine refill on grenade use. In the second column, Gear Shift provides up to ×5 stacks for 15s on reloads following a weapon kill or becoming Amplified, providing up to max ×1.35 damage. The Cursed Thrall origin trait synergises well with melee builds - killing an enemy with a melee will proc a 4.5m radius Cursed Thrall AoE explosion for 7.5s (8.5s enhanced) on the next weapon kill. If you're interested, an edict is an official order or decree issued by an authority. So the name implies that the weapon acts as a manifested extension of the Hive Oversoul's power, enforcing the absolute, violent decree of the Hive gods. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Veillantif-D - PvE Boss god 1
// (Quick Launch, Smart Drift Control, Hard Launch, Volatile Launch), (Tactical Mag, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Air Trigger), (Incandescent, Burning Ambition)
//notes:Inspired by @Saint_Kabr and @ImpetusAlways (PvE PvE-Boss PvE-BurstDamage PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for burst DPS against a single orange or yellow-bar target, such as a Bane, Elite, Champion or Miniboss in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty, particularly with solar builds. Veillantif-D is a Micro-Missile pulse rifle which as a subfamily are currently rated S-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ providing "game-warping levels of add clear, nearly 2x damage per brick vs. special ammo HC, forgiving crit multi". The frame intrinsic offers built-in anti-Unstoppable and micro-missile projectiles that track locked-on targets as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are decent by comparison with its peers - you can see them at https://destiny.report/w/1361871430/veillantif-d and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/1361871430 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. Look for a barrel perk that improves Handling, Velocity or maybe a bit of Blast Radius, and then with the magazine perk go for something that helps improve magazine size or reload. For burst damage against beefier enemies, in the first trait perk column Air Trigger, passively boosts Ammo Generation by +20 (+30 when enhanced) as well as Reload Speed by +30 with a ×0.8 Reload Animation Scalar, ×0.85 Accuracy Cone Size and ×0.8 Accuracy Cone Growth while airborne (+35 Reload Speed and ×0.8 Accuracy Cone size enhanced). In the second column, for solar synergy, Incandescent spreads +30 Solar Scorch stacks within 4m on a red bar Minor add kill (+40 stacks within 8m for orange and yellow bars, +45 when enhanced), with scorch damage counting as weapon damage so can inherit any weapon damage bonuses. Alternatively, Burning Ambition works better to build Solar ignitions on tougher targets if you have a solar ability to apply scorch initially, otherwise it'll take 3 hits (2 when enhanced) within 2s of each other to apply +20 scorch stacks with +20 added on each additional hit to build towards an ignition. All of the scorch stacks are increased by ×1.5 with Ember of Ashes. The Featherweight origin trait stacks up to ×10 to improve movement speed, or Häkke Breach Armaments passively boosts damage against vehicles by ×1.15 (×1.2 enhanced), turrets and constructs by ×1.3 (×1.6 enhanced) and Stasis crystals by ×1.45. If you're interested, in the "Matter of France" (a collection of medieval legends similar to King Arthur's tales), Veillantif is the legendary, highly intelligent warhorse of the paladin Roland. It's also a nod to the original Marathon game, where the character Durandal makes frequent references to Roland's mythos. Recommended MW: Handling or Velocity, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Boss PvE-BurstDamage PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Veillantif-D - PvE Boss god 2
// (Quick Launch, Smart Drift Control, Hard Launch, Volatile Launch), (Tactical Mag, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Air Trigger), (Paracausal Affinity, Aggregate Charge)
//notes:Inspired by @Saint_Kabr and @ImpetusAlways (PvE PvE-Boss PvE-BurstDamage PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for burst DPS against a single orange or yellow-bar target, such as a Bane, Elite, Champion or Miniboss in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Veillantif-D is a Micro-Missile pulse rifle which as a subfamily are currently rated S-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ providing "game-warping levels of add clear, nearly 2x damage per brick vs. special ammo HC, forgiving crit multi". The frame intrinsic offers built-in anti-Unstoppable and micro-missile projectiles that track locked-on targets as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are decent by comparison with its peers - you can see them at https://destiny.report/w/1361871430/veillantif-d and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/1361871430 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. Look for a barrel perk that improves Handling, Velocity or maybe a bit of Blast Radius, and then with the magazine perk go for something that helps improve magazine size or reload. For burst damage against beefier enemies, in the first trait perk column Air Trigger, passively boosts Ammo Generation by +20 (+30 when enhanced) as well as Reload Speed by +30 with a ×0.8 Reload Animation Scalar, ×0.85 Accuracy Cone Size and ×0.8 Accuracy Cone Growth while airborne (+35 Reload Speed and ×0.8 Accuracy Cone size enhanced). In the second column, for additional damage, Paracausal Affinity boosts damage by ×1.2 for 6s after any kill of a Light energy type, or Aggregate Charge provides a stack for each different elemental debuff up to ×3 stacks to boost damage by a max ×1.3 (×1.33 enhanced), with each stack lasting as long as the debuff is active. The Featherweight origin trait stacks up to ×10 to improve movement speed, or Häkke Breach Armaments passively boosts damage against vehicles by ×1.15 (×1.2 enhanced), turrets and constructs by ×1.3 (×1.6 enhanced) and Stasis crystals by ×1.45. If you're interested, in the "Matter of France" (a collection of medieval legends similar to King Arthur's tales), Veillantif is the legendary, highly intelligent warhorse of the paladin Roland. It's also a nod to the original Marathon game, where the character Durandal makes frequent references to Roland's mythos. Recommended MW: Handling or Velocity, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Boss PvE-BurstDamage PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Veillantif-D - PvE Boss god 3
// (Volatile Launch, Smart Drift Control, Hard Launch, Quick Launch, Smart Drift Control), (Tactical Mag, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Blast Distributor), (Incandescent, Burning Ambition)
//notes:Inspired by @Saint_Kabr and @ImpetusAlways (PvE PvE-Boss PvE-BurstDamage PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for burst DPS against a single orange or yellow-bar target, such as a Bane, Elite, Champion or Miniboss in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty, particularly for Solar grenade builds. Veillantif-D is a Micro-Missile pulse rifle which as a subfamily are currently rated S-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ providing "game-warping levels of add clear, nearly 2x damage per brick vs. special ammo HC, forgiving crit multi". The frame intrinsic offers built-in anti-Unstoppable and micro-missile projectiles that track locked-on targets as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are decent by comparison with its peers - you can see them at https://destiny.report/w/1361871430/veillantif-d and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/1361871430 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. Look for a barrel perk that improves Blast Radius, or maybe a bit of Handling or Velocity, and then with the magazine perk go for something that helps improve magazine size or reload. For burst damage against beefier enemies, in the first trait perk column Blast Distributor stacks up to ×5 to provide +10 to your Grenade attribute (+13 when enhanced) per stack on explosive damage to a max of +50 (+65 enhanced) for a refreshable 5s on further explosive damage so you can invest stat points into other armour attributes. You can use solar grenades to apply scorch to build towards Solar ignitions on tougher targets using Burning Ambition in the second column. Without a grenade to prime scorch, it'll take 3 hits (2 when enhanced) within 2s of each other to apply +20 scorch stacks, with +20 added on each additional hit to build towards an ignition. All of the scorch stacks are increased by ×1.5 with Ember of Ashes. The Featherweight origin trait stacks up to ×10 to improve movement speed, or Häkke Breach Armaments passively boosts damage against vehicles by ×1.15 (×1.2 enhanced), turrets and constructs by ×1.3 (×1.6 enhanced) and Stasis crystals by ×1.45. If you're interested, in the "Matter of France" (a collection of medieval legends similar to King Arthur's tales), Veillantif is the legendary, highly intelligent warhorse of the paladin Roland. It's also a nod to the original Marathon game, where the character Durandal makes frequent references to Roland's mythos. Recommended MW: Blast Radius, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Boss PvE-BurstDamage PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Veillantif-D - PvE Boss god 1
// (Quick Launch, Smart Drift Control, Hard Launch, Volatile Launch), (Tactical Mag, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Air Trigger), (Incandescent, Burning Ambition)
//notes:Inspired by @Saint_Kabr and @ImpetusAlways (PvE PvE-Boss PvE-BurstDamage PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for burst DPS against a single orange or yellow-bar target, such as a Bane, Elite, Champion or Miniboss in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty, particularly with solar builds. Veillantif-D is a Micro-Missile pulse rifle which as a subfamily are currently rated S-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ providing "game-warping levels of add clear, nearly 2x damage per brick vs. special ammo HC, forgiving crit multi". The frame intrinsic offers built-in anti-Unstoppable and micro-missile projectiles that track locked-on targets as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are decent by comparison with its peers - you can see them at https://destiny.report/w/406384293/veillantif-d and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/406384293 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. Look for a barrel perk that improves Handling, Velocity or maybe a bit of Blast Radius, and then with the magazine perk go for something that helps improve magazine size or reload. For burst damage against beefier enemies, in the first trait perk column Air Trigger, passively boosts Ammo Generation by +20 (+30 when enhanced) as well as Reload Speed by +30 with a ×0.8 Reload Animation Scalar, ×0.85 Accuracy Cone Size and ×0.8 Accuracy Cone Growth while airborne (+35 Reload Speed and ×0.8 Accuracy Cone size enhanced). In the second column, for solar synergy, Incandescent spreads +30 Solar Scorch stacks within 4m on a red bar Minor add kill (+40 stacks within 8m for orange and yellow bars, +45 when enhanced), with scorch damage counting as weapon damage so can inherit any weapon damage bonuses. Alternatively, Burning Ambition works better to build Solar ignitions on tougher targets if you have a solar ability to apply scorch initially, otherwise it'll take 3 hits (2 when enhanced) within 2s of each other to apply +20 scorch stacks with +20 added on each additional hit to build towards an ignition. All of the scorch stacks are increased by ×1.5 with Ember of Ashes. The Featherweight origin trait stacks up to ×10 to improve movement speed, or Häkke Breach Armaments passively boosts damage against vehicles by ×1.15 (×1.2 enhanced), turrets and constructs by ×1.3 (×1.6 enhanced) and Stasis crystals by ×1.45. If you're interested, in the "Matter of France" (a collection of medieval legends similar to King Arthur's tales), Veillantif is the legendary, highly intelligent warhorse of the paladin Roland. It's also a nod to the original Marathon game, where the character Durandal makes frequent references to Roland's mythos. Recommended MW: Handling or Velocity, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Boss PvE-BurstDamage PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

You can use solar grenades to apply scorch to build towards Solar ignitions on tougher targets using Burning Ambition in the second column. Without a grenade to prime scorch, it'll take 3 hits (2 when enhanced) within 2s of each other to apply +20 scorch stacks, with +20 added on each additional hit to build towards an ignition. All of the scorch stacks are increased by ×1.5 with Ember of Ashes. The Featherweight origin trait stacks up to ×10 to improve movement speed, or Häkke Breach Armaments passively boosts damage against vehicles by ×1.15 (×1.2 enhanced), turrets and constructs by ×1.3 (×1.6 enhanced) and Stasis crystals by ×1.45. // Battle Scar - PvE Minor god 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling), (Tactical Mag, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Shoot to Loot, Attrition Orbs), (Kinetic Tremors)
//notes:Inspired by @Saint_Kabr and @ImpetusAlways (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing groups of Minor adds in activities rated Normal, Hard and Very Hard difficulty. The reprised Battle Scar is a Lightweight pulse rifle which as a subfamily are currently rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ as "essentially just a worse high-impact auto". The frame intrinsic offers built-in anti-Overload, and +20 to the hidden Mobility stat and ×1.0625 base movement speed as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are decent by comparison with its peers - you can see the stats at https://destiny.report/w/1525080480/battle-scar and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/1525080480 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil is vertical but loose, so to tighten that up, look for a barrel perk that helps with Recoil Direction or, if you can handle it just go for extra handling, and then with the magazine perk go for something that helps improve magazine size or reload. For clearing groups of adds, in the second trait perk column Kinetic Tremors emits three 6m AoE Kinetic shockwaves after 11 hits (10 when enhanced) within 3s of each other, which are treated as weapon damage so inherit damage boosts. You can pair either Shoot to Loot, which lets you pick up remote ammo bricks or orbs from safety by shooting them, which also reloads all your weapons from reserves or Attrition Orbs spawns a power orb after ~28 hits within 5s of each other to proc armour mods - both have their range of effect extended by the kinetic shockwaves. The Veteran's Wisdom origin trait procs on a crit kill or 3 kills within 3s of each other to generate an 8m (9m when enhanced) AoE kinetic shockwave which inflicts Exhaust on enemies, or there's Field-Tested which starts subtly but if you can stay alive to get kills, you'll start to feel the gunfeel stat boosts as you get to the higher stacks. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

The reprised Battle Scar is a Lightweight pulse rifle which as a subfamily are currently rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ as "essentially just a worse high-impact auto". The frame intrinsic offers built-in anti-Overload, and +20 to the hidden Mobility stat and ×1.0625 base movement speed as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are decent by comparison with its peers - you can see the stats at https://destiny.report/w/1525080480/battle-scar and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/1525080480 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. If you're using the Kinetic Synthesis artifact mod, then picking up the kinetic ammo or a normal special brick will proc both All-Star to boosts damage by ×1.35 and Reload Speed by +100 for a refreshable 6s (7s enhanced), or 9s (10s enhanced) for a heavy brick, with the timer starting only after you ready, or also Adhesive Ordnance generates a Sticky Bomb from thin air, which detonates 0.8s after impact for 6m AoE damage plus applying exhaust for 5s - the explosion is treated as weapon damage so inherits damage boosts and also procs weapon effects like Attrition Orbs. The Veteran's Wisdom origin trait procs on a crit kill or 3 kills within 3s of each other to generate an 8m (9m when enhanced) AoE kinetic shockwave which inflicts Exhaust on enemies, or there's Field-Tested which starts subtly but if you can stay alive to get kills, you'll start to feel the gunfeel stat boosts as you get to the higher stacks. // Battle Scar - PvE Minor god 3
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling), (Tactical Mag, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Shoot to Loot, Attrition Orbs), (Kinetic Tremors)
//notes:Inspired by @Saint_Kabr and @ImpetusAlways (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing groups of Minor adds in activities rated Normal, Hard and Very Hard difficulty. The reprised Battle Scar is a Lightweight pulse rifle which as a subfamily are currently rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ as "essentially just a worse high-impact auto". The frame intrinsic offers built-in anti-Overload, and +20 to the hidden Mobility stat and ×1.0625 base movement speed as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are decent by comparison with its peers - you can see the stats at https://destiny.report/w/1525080480/battle-scar and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/1525080480 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil is vertical but loose, so to tighten that up, look for a barrel perk that helps with Recoil Direction or, if you can handle it just go for extra handling, and then with the magazine perk go for something that helps improve magazine size or reload. For dealing with tougher individual adds, in the first trait perk column Shoot to Loot lets you pick up remote ammo bricks or orbs from safety by shooting them, which also reloads all your weapons from reserves. In the second, for damage, Adhesive Ordnance generates a Sticky Bomb from thin air when you pickup a kinetic brick dropped by the Kinetic Synthesis artefact mod or spcial brick, which detonates 0.8s after impact for 6m AoE damage plus applying exhaust for 5s - the explosion is treated as weapon damage so inherits damage boosts and also procs weapon effects Shoot to Loot to extend its pickup area. The Veteran's Wisdom origin trait procs on a crit kill or 3 kills within 3s of each other to generate an 8m (9m when enhanced) AoE kinetic shockwave which inflicts Exhaust on enemies, or there's Field-Tested which starts subtly but if you can stay alive to get kills, you'll start to feel the gunfeel stat boosts as you get to the higher stacks. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller
