// Corrasion - PvE Minor god 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling), (Tactical Mag, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Eddy Current, Enlightened Action), (Voltshot)
//notes:Inspired by @Saint_Kabr and @ImpetusAlways (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing add groups in activities rated Normal difficulty, particularly with Arc subclasses. The updated Corrasion is a Heavy Burst pulse rifle which as a subfamily are currently rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ as "essentially just a worse high-impact auto". The frame intrinsic offers built-in anti-Unstoppable, fires a 2-round burst and grants ×0.9 flinch when ADS as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are okay by comparison with its peers - you can see the stats at https://destiny.report/w/2126178511/corrasion hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/2126178511 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil is left-ish and a little loose, so to tighten that up, look for a barrel perk that helps with Recoil Direction or, if you can handle it just go for extra handling, and then with the magazine perk go for something that helps improve magazine size or reload. For dealing with add groups and synergising with arc builds, in the first trait perk column, Eddy Current boosts Handling with a ×0.95 animation scalar and +20 Reload Speed with a ×0.95 animation scalar for 3s after sprinting for 1.5s, which is all applied instantly with +60 to Reload Speed when Amplified, or Enlightened Action builds stacks on hits for a refreshable 2s that build after 50 hits (17 bursts) to +50 Handling and Reload Speed (+55 when enhanced). For damage, Voltshot applies Arc Jolt to the next target you hit within 7s (8s when enhanced), after a reload within 5.3s of a kill, for additional damage to the target on further hits plus AoE chain lightning damage on nearby enemies. The Radiolaria Transposer origin trait is useful for clearing adds - three Minors or one Major or Boss enemy kill will result in a 3m AoE explosion on death, leaving a damaging pool of radiolaria for a few seconds. The damage is treated as weapon damage so inherits damage boosts. If you're interested, corrasion is a mechanical form of erosion where moving elements - such as water, wind, or ice - carry abrasive particles that continuously scrape, grind, and wear away a surface. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller

//notes:Inspired by @Saint_Kabr and @ImpetusAlways (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Very Hard, Hard or Normal difficulty. The updated Corrasion is a Heavy Burst pulse rifle which as a subfamily are currently rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ as "essentially just a worse high-impact auto". The frame intrinsic offers built-in anti-Unstoppable, fires a 2-round burst and grants ×0.9 flinch when ADS as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are okay by comparison with its peers - you can see the stats at https://destiny.report/w/2126178511/corrasion hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/2126178511 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil is left-ish and a little loose, so to tighten that up, look for a barrel perk that helps with Recoil Direction or, if you can handle it just go for extra handling, and then with the magazine perk go for something that helps improve magazine size or reload. For dealing with tougher single adds, there's the double perk combination of High Impact Reserves, which boosts damage from ×1.121 to ×1.256 as the magazine drops below 50% (55% when enhanced) togeter with Frenzy which, after being in combat for 12s - that's dealing or taking damage - boosts Handling and Reload Speed by +100 and boosts damage by ×1.15 for a refreshable 7.2s. The Radiolaria Transposer origin trait is useful for clearing adds - three Minors or one Major or Boss enemy kill will result in a 3m AoE explosion on death, leaving a damaging pool of radiolaria for a few seconds. The damage is treated as weapon damage so inherits damage boosts. If you're interested, corrasion is a mechanical form of erosion where moving elements - such as water, wind, or ice - carry abrasive particles that continuously scrape, grind, and wear away a surface. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Joxer's Longsword - PvE Minor god 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling), (High-Caliber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Repulsor Brace), (Destabilizing Rounds, Demoralize, Withering Gaze)
//notes:Inspired by @Saint_Kabr and @ImpetusAlways (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing add groups in activities rated Normal difficulty, particularly with Void subclasses. The reprised Joxer's Longsword is a Heavy Burst pulse rifle which as a subfamily are currently rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ as "essentially just a worse high-impact auto". The frame intrinsic offers built-in anti-Unstoppable, fires a 2-round burst and grants ×0.9 flinch when ADS as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are okay by comparison with its peers - you can see the stats at https://destiny.report/w/2150012406/joxers-longsword hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/2150012406 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil is right-ish and a little loose, so to tighten that up, look for a barrel perk that helps with Recoil Direction or, if you can handle it just go for extra handling, and then with the magazine perk go for one of the combat effect mags or something that helps improve magazine size or reload. For dealing with add groups and synergising with Void builds, in the first trait perk column, Repulsor Brace provides a ×0.5 damage resisting Void overshield for 8s (10s when enhanced) after killing a void-debuffed enemy - duration boosted by ×1.5 with Echo of Persistence. In the second column you have the full set of void perks - Destabilizing Rounds spreads Void Volatility on a kill in a 6.5m radius and provides Volatile Rounds for 4.5s (5.25s when enhanced), Demoralize spreads a ~4m AoE cloud of Void Weaken to nearby enemies for a ×1.15 boost to damage for your fireteam for 3s (5s when enhanced) on a crit kill or Withering Gaze applies the Void Weaken debuff to targets after ADS-ing for 1.4s (1.3s enhanced) for ×1.15 damage for a refreshable 6s for your fireteam, but if the stronger ×1.3 weaken has been applied from Tractor Cannon or Shadowshot, a proc will reapply at the higher value. There's a choice of origin traits - Roar of Battle grants +10 Stability and Handling for 6s after dealing or taking damage every 0.5s or so for 2s or Nadir Focus grants stacks for 2s for each shot fired to boost Range by up to +10 and Accuracy Cone Size to ×0.925 after ~4 bursts. If you're interested, Joxer's Longsword gets its name from Joxer, a veteran, but tragic Titan whose storyline is one of the most notoriously heroic - and foolhardy - tales in Destiny's lore. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller

The reprised Joxer's Longsword is a Heavy Burst pulse rifle which as a subfamily are currently rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ as "essentially just a worse high-impact auto". The frame intrinsic offers built-in anti-Unstoppable, fires a 2-round burst and grants ×0.9 flinch when ADS as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil is right-ish and a little loose, so to tighten that up, look for a barrel perk that helps with Recoil Direction or, if you can handle it just go for extra handling, and then with the magazine perk go for one of the combat effect mags or something that helps improve magazine size or reload. For dealing with add groups and synergising with Void builds, in the first trait perk column, Dragonfly procs a 4.5m Void explosion on a crit kill - this counts as weapon damage so can inherit damage boosts. In the second column you have Void perks for a double damage roll - Destabilizing Rounds spreads Void Volatility on a kill in a 6.5m radius and provides Volatile Rounds for 4.5s (5.25s when enhanced), or Demoralize spreads a ~4m AoE cloud of Void Weaken to nearby enemies for a ×1.15 boost to damage for your fireteam for 3s (5s when enhanced) on a crit kill. If you're interested, Joxer's Longsword gets its name from Joxer, a veteran, but tragic Titan whose storyline is one of the most notoriously heroic - and foolhardy - tales in Destiny's lore. The recoil is right-ish and a little loose, so to tighten that up, look for a barrel perk that helps with Recoil Direction or, if you can handle it just go for extra handling, and then with the magazine perk go for one of the combat effect mags or something that helps improve magazine size or reload. For dealing with tougher individual adds, and to synergise with grenade builds, there's the tried and trusted trait perk combination of Demolitionist to generate chunk grenade ability energy on kills and an instant magazine refill on grenade use and Adrenaline Junkie which generates up to ×5 stacks on kills for +20 handling and ×1.33 damage, with a grenade kill giving an instant 5 stacks, for a refreshable 4.5s (5s when enhanced). There's a choice of origin traits - Roar of Battle grants +10 Stability and Handling for 6s after dealing or taking damage every 0.5s or so for 2s or Nadir Focus grants stacks for 2s for each shot fired to boost Range by up to +10 and Accuracy Cone Size to ×0.925 after ~4 bursts. If you're interested, Joxer's Longsword gets its name from Joxer, a veteran, but tragic Titan whose storyline is one of the most notoriously heroic - and foolhardy - tales in Destiny's lore. // Joxer's Longsword - PvE Minor god 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling), (High-Caliber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Repulsor Brace), (Destabilizing Rounds, Demoralize, Withering Gaze)
//notes:Inspired by @Saint_Kabr and @ImpetusAlways (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing add groups in activities rated Normal difficulty, particularly with Void subclasses. The reprised Joxer's Longsword is a Heavy Burst pulse rifle which as a subfamily are currently rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ as "essentially just a worse high-impact auto". The frame intrinsic offers built-in anti-Unstoppable, fires a 2-round burst and grants ×0.9 flinch when ADS as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are okay by comparison with its peers - you can see the stats at https://destiny.report/w/3538003989/joxers-longsword hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/3538003989 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil is right-ish and a little loose, so to tighten that up, look for a barrel perk that helps with Recoil Direction or, if you can handle it just go for extra handling, and then with the magazine perk go for one of the combat effect mags or something that helps improve magazine size or reload. For dealing with add groups and synergising with Void builds, in the first trait perk column, Repulsor Brace provides a ×0.5 damage resisting Void overshield for 8s (10s when enhanced) after killing a void-debuffed enemy - duration boosted by ×1.5 with Echo of Persistence. In the second column you have the full set of void perks - Destabilizing Rounds spreads Void Volatility on a kill in a 6.5m radius and provides Volatile Rounds for 4.5s (5.25s when enhanced), Demoralize spreads a ~4m AoE cloud of Void Weaken to nearby enemies for a ×1.15 boost to damage for your fireteam for 3s (5s when enhanced) on a crit kill or Withering Gaze applies the Void Weaken debuff to targets after ADS-ing for 1.4s (1.3s enhanced) for ×1.15 damage for a refreshable 6s for your fireteam, but if the stronger ×1.3 weaken has been applied from Tractor Cannon or Shadowshot, a proc will reapply at the higher value. There's a choice of origin traits - Roar of Battle grants +10 Stability and Handling for 6s after dealing or taking damage every 0.5s or so for 2s or Nadir Focus grants stacks for 2s for each shot fired to boost Range by up to +10 and Accuracy Cone Size to ×0.925 after ~4 bursts. If you're interested, Joxer's Longsword gets its name from Joxer, a veteran, but tragic Titan whose storyline is one of the most notoriously heroic - and foolhardy - tales in Destiny's lore. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Joxer's Longsword - PvE Minor god 3
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling), (High-Caliber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Demolitionist), (Adrenaline Junkie)
//notes:Inspired by @Saint_Kabr and @ImpetusAlways (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal difficulty, particularly with grenade builds. The reprised Joxer's Longsword is a Heavy Burst pulse rifle which as a subfamily are currently rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ as "essentially just a worse high-impact auto". The frame intrinsic offers built-in anti-Unstoppable, fires a 2-round burst and grants ×0.9 flinch when ADS as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are okay by comparison with its peers - you can see the stats at https://destiny.report/w/3538003989/joxers-longsword hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/3538003989  will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil is right-ish and a little loose, so to tighten that up, look for a barrel perk that helps with Recoil Direction or, if you can handle it just go for extra handling, and then with the magazine perk go for one of the combat effect mags or something that helps improve magazine size or reload. For dealing with tougher individual adds, and to synergise with grenade builds, there's the tried and trusted trait perk combination of Demolitionist to generate chunk grenade ability energy on kills and an instant magazine refill on grenade use and Adrenaline Junkie which generates up to ×5 stacks on kills for +20 handling and ×1.33 damage, with a grenade kill giving an instant 5 stacks, for a refreshable 4.5s (5s when enhanced). There's a choice of origin traits - Roar of Battle grants +10 Stability and Handling for 6s after dealing or taking damage every 0.5s or so for 2s or Nadir Focus grants stacks for 2s for each shot fired to boost Range by up to +10 and Accuracy Cone Size to ×0.925 after ~4 bursts. If you're interested, Joxer's Longsword gets its name from Joxer, a veteran, but tragic Titan whose storyline is one of the most notoriously heroic - and foolhardy - tales in Destiny's lore. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller

// The Time-Worn Spire - PvE Minor god 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling), (High-Caliber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Alloy Mag, Appended Mag, Flared Magwell), (Firefly), (Headstone, Crystalline Corpsebloom)
//notes:Inspired by @Saint_Kabr and @ImpetusAlways (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing groups of Minor adds in activities rated Normal difficulty, particularly with Stasis builds. The Time-Worn Spire returns as a Rapid-Fire pulse rifle which as a subfamily are currently rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ as "essentially just a worse high-impact auto". The frame intrinsic offers built-in anti-Overload, and a ×0.8 reload animation multiplier when starting a reload with an empty mag as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are decent by comparison with its peers - you can see the stats at https://destiny.report/w/2204165992/the-time-worn-spire and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/2204165992 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil is right and loose, so to tighten that up, look for a barrel perk that helps with Recoil Direction or, if you can handle it just go for extra handling, and then with the magazine perk go for any of the combat effect mags or something that helps improve magazine size or reload. For Stasis synergy and clearing groups of adds in general content, in the first trait perk column Firefly procs a 4m AoE Solar explosion on a crit kill plus +50 Reload Speed for 6s. In the second column, Headstone generates a Stasis crystal on a crit kill, or Crystalline Corpsebloom procs on a kill to provide a 4s buff - kills with the buff active generate a Stasis seeker which travels 3m before spawning a medium Stasis crystal (large when enhanced). The Stasis crystals are weapon-generated entities so can inherit damage buffs, and proc Stasis subclass fragments when shattered for AoE damage. Handily, Firefly crit kill explosions will instantly shatter Headstone crystals, and nearby Corpsebloom crystals to help clear add groups. The Skulking Wolf origin trait can be useful, proccing after you get a kill at ≤90 HP, enhancing radar and making adds less accurate when firing at you for 10s. If you're interested, the name references "Silimar's Spire," a massive, imposing tower in the Iron Temple built by him as a monument to unity. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller

//notes:Inspired by @Saint_Kabr and @ImpetusAlways (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing groups of Minor adds in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty, particularly with Prismatic builds. The Time-Worn Spire returns as a Rapid-Fire pulse rifle which as a subfamily are currently rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ as "essentially just a worse high-impact auto". The frame intrinsic offers built-in anti-Overload, and a ×0.8 reload animation multiplier when starting a reload with an empty mag as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. For clearing groups of adds in endgame content, in the first trait perk column Firefly procs a 4m AoE Solar explosion on a crit kill plus +50 Reload Speed for 6s. In the second column, to synergise with Prismatic builds, Elemental Honing grants an escalating stack of damage up to ×1.35 for 20s (23s when enhanced) for each elemental damage type inflicted, which can be refreshed with a new damage type until you've run through all 5. Handily, a crit kill that inflicts solar damage through Firefly combined with the stasis damage type of the weapon get you to an instant ×2 stacks to start you off. The Skulking Wolf origin trait can be useful, proccing after you get a kill at ≤90 HP, enhancing radar and making adds less accurate when firing at you for 10s. If you're interested, the name references "Silimar's Spire," a massive, imposing tower in the Iron Temple built by him as a monument to unity. // Darkest Before - PvE Minor god 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling), (High-Caliber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Heal Clip), (Incandescent)
//notes:Inspired by @Saint_Kabr and @ImpetusAlways (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing groups of Minor adds in activities rated Normal difficulty, particularly with Solar builds. The reprised Darkest Before is a Rapid-Fire pulse rifle which as a subfamily are currently rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ as "essentially just a worse high-impact auto". The frame intrinsic offers built-in anti-Overload, and a ×0.8 reload animation multiplier when starting a reload with an empty mag as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are pretty much best in class by comparison with its peers - you can see the stats at https://destiny.report/w/859869931/nullify and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/859869931 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil is left and a little loose, so to tighten that up, look for a barrel perk that helps with Recoil Direction or, if you can handle it, just go for extra Handling, and then with the magazine perk go for something that helps improve magazine size or reload. For solar build synergy, there's the fan-favourite trait perk combinations of Heal Clip to provide ×2 Solar Cure (140 HP), and ×1 Cure on allies within 15m, when you finish a reload within ~5.3s (~5.5s when enhanced) after a kill, or Attrition Orbs spawns a power orb after 26 hits within 5s of each other to proc armour mods together with Incandescent which spreads +30 Solar Scorch stacks within 4m on a red bar Minor add kill (+40 stacks within 8m for orange and yellow bars, +45 when enhanced). The scorch damage counting as weapon damage so can inherit any weapon damage bonuses and proc weapon effects like Attrition Orbs. All of the scorch stacks are increased by ×1.5 with Ember of Ashes. The Crossing Over origin trait is a little weird, providing extra range and handling in the top half of the mag, and a small damage boost in the bottom starting at ×1.015 at 50% and ×1.03 with your penultimate and last shot. If you're interested, "Darkest Before" is a play on the classic proverb, "It is always darkest before the dawn" - darkness being the absolute peak of a crisis, just before a breakthrough or resolution, when light appears. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller

//notes:Inspired by @Saint_Kabr and @ImpetusAlways (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-God PvE-EndGame M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Very Hard, Hard and Normal difficulty. The reprised Darkest Before is a Rapid-Fire pulse rifle which as a subfamily are currently rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ as "essentially just a worse high-impact auto". The frame intrinsic offers built-in anti-Overload, and a ×0.8 reload animation multiplier when starting a reload with an empty mag as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The recoil is left and a little loose, so to tighten that up, look for a barrel perk that helps with Recoil Direction or, if you can handle it, just go for extra Handling, and then with the magazine perk go for something that helps improve magazine size or reload. For endgame use, Attrition Orbs in the first trait perk column spawns a power orb after 26 hits within 5s of each other to proc armour mods. In the second column, One For All boosts damage by ×1.35 for a non-refreshable 10s (11s when enhanced) after hitting three separate targets within 3s of each, or Focused Fury boosts damage by ×1.25 for a refreshable 11s (12s enhanced) or until reload or stow, after hitting 35% of the mag - as precision hits that's ~13. The Crossing Over origin trait is a little weird, providing extra range and handling in the top half of the mag, and a small damage boost in the bottom starting at ×1.015 at 50% and ×1.03 with your penultimate and last shot. If you're interested, "Darkest Before" is a play on the classic proverb, "It is always darkest before the dawn" - darkness being the absolute peak of a crisis, just before a breakthrough or resolution, when light appears. //notes:Inspired by @Saint_Kabr and @ImpetusAlways (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-God PvE-EndGame M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Very Hard, Hard and Normal difficulty, particularly on Solar builds. The reprised Darkest Before is a Rapid-Fire pulse rifle which as a subfamily are currently rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ as "essentially just a worse high-impact auto". // Darkest Before - PvE Minor god 4
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling), (High-Caliber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Attrition Orbs), (Burning Ambition)
//notes:Inspired by @Saint_Kabr and @ImpetusAlways (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-God PvE-EndGame M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Very Hard, Hard and Normal difficulty, particularly on Prismatic builds. The reprised Darkest Before is a Rapid-Fire pulse rifle which as a subfamily are currently rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ as "essentially just a worse high-impact auto". The frame intrinsic offers built-in anti-Overload, and a ×0.8 reload animation multiplier when starting a reload with an empty mag as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are pretty much best in class by comparison with its peers - you can see the stats at https://destiny.report/w/859869931/nullify and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/859869931 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil is left and a little loose, so to tighten that up, look for a barrel perk that helps with Recoil Direction or, if you can handle it, just go for extra Handling, and then with the magazine perk go for something that helps improve magazine size or reload. For endgame use, Attrition Orbs in the first trait perk column spawns a power orb after 26 hits within 5s of each other to proc armour mods. In the second column, for Prismatic synergy, Elemental Honing grants an escalating stack of damage up to ×1.35 max for 20s (23s when enhanced) for each elemental damage type inflicted, which can be refreshed with a new damage type until you've run through all 5. The Crossing Over origin trait is a little weird, providing extra range and handling in the top half of the mag, and a small damage boost in the bottom starting at ×1.015 at 50% and ×1.03 with your penultimate and last shot. If you're interested, "Darkest Before" is a play on the classic proverb, "It is always darkest before the dawn" - darkness being the absolute peak of a crisis, just before a breakthrough or resolution, when light appears. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-God PvE-EndGame M+KB Controller

// Adhortative - PvE Utility god 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling), (Armor-Piercing Rounds, High-Caliber Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Attrition Orbs, Shoot to Loot), (Disruption Break)
//notes:Inspired by @Saint_Kabr and @ImpetusAlways (PvE PvE-Utility PvE-Champion PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for piercing Barrier Champions in activities rated Very Hard, Hard or Normal difficulty. The updated Adhortative is an Adaptive pulse rifle which as a subfamily are currently rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ as "small step down from C tier pulse damage profile, but has barrier intrinsic". The frame intrinsic offers no bonuses beyond being "well-rounded, reliable and sturdy", as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are on the low side by comparison with its peers - you can see the stats at https://destiny.report/w/3744058588/the-third-axiom and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/3744058588 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil is a little left and a bit loose, so to tighten that up, look for a barrel perk that helps with Recoil Direction or, if you can handle it just go for extra handling, and then with the magazine perk go for Armor-Piercing Rounds for the additional ×1.05 shield damage, one of the other combat effect mags or something that helps improve magazine size or reload. This is, admittedly, a niche anti-Barrier roll but in the first column you could look for Attrition Orbs to spawn a power orb after ~26 hits within 5s of each other to proc armour mods or Shoot to Loot which lets you pick up remote ammo bricks or orbs from safety by shooting them, which also reloads all your weapons from reserves - both are always useful. In the second column, Disruption Break increases kinetic damage by ×1.5 for 5.5s after breaking an elemental shield or barrier, you should be able to quickly finish off Barrier Champions from a safe distance with a kinetic special weapon. The Nano-Munitions origin trait grants a stack up to ×7 for being within 15m of an ally, using a stack to refill 25% of your primary or secondary weapon mag when empty. If you're interested, "Adhortative" relates to the giving of advice, encouragement, or strong urging, such as saying "Okay everyone, let's wipe, rally and have another try". Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Utility PvE-Champion PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Infinite Paths 8 - PvE Minor god 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling), (Tactical Mag, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Trickle Charge, Supercharged Magazine, Demolitionist), (Rolling Storm)
//notes:Inspired by @Saint_Kabr and @ImpetusAlways (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal difficulty, particularly with Arc subclasses. The reprised Infinite Paths 8 is a Lightweight pulse rifle which as a subfamily are currently rated D-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ as "essentially just a worse high-impact auto". The frame intrinsic offers built-in anti-Overload, and +20 to the hidden Mobility stat and ×1.0625 base movement speed as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are on the low side by comparison with its peers - you can see the stats at https://destiny.report/w/3176697588/infinite-paths-8 hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/3176697588 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil is right and quite loose, so to tighten that up, look for a barrel perk that helps with Recoil Direction or, if you can handle it just go for extra handling, and then with the magazine perk go for something that helps improve magazine size or reload. For single target damage, synergising with arc builds, in the first trait perk column, Trickle Charge refills 10% of the magazine on receiving a stack of Arc Bolt Charge, and 20% when you discharge with a lightning bolt on a target, or Supercharged Magazine refills 5% of the magazine every 1.5s while you're Amplified or, after you sprint when Amplified to gain Speed Booster this accelerates to 5% every 0.25s while the buff is active, overflowing the magazine to up to ×2 capacity. Alternatively, for your neutral game, Demolitionist generates chunk grenade ability energy on kills and an instant magazine refill on grenade use. In the second column, for damage, Rolling Storm generates ×1 stack of Arc Bolt Charge (×2 when Amplified) on a kill, to trigger a lightning bolt at ×10 stacks for both target and AoE damage. The Radiolaria Transposer origin trait is useful for clearing adds - three Minors or one Major or Boss enemy kill will result in a 3m AoE explosion on death, leaving a damaging pool of radiolaria for a few seconds. The damage is treated as weapon damage so inherits damage boosts. If you're interested, "Infinite Paths," refers to the Infinite Forest Vex simulation engine on Mercury, while the "8" references Lost Prophecy, Verse 8 - the specific algorithm or riddle that Brother Vance gave us to forge the weapon back in Curse of Osiris. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Infinite Paths 8 - PvE Minor god 2
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling), (Tactical Mag, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Trickle Charge, Supercharged Magazine, Dragonfly, Demolitionist), (Voltshot)
//notes:Inspired by @Saint_Kabr and @ImpetusAlways (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing add groups in activities rated Normal difficulty, particularly with Arc subclasses. The reprised Infinite Paths 8 is a Lightweight pulse rifle which as a subfamily are currently rated D-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ as "essentially just a worse high-impact auto". The frame intrinsic offers built-in anti-Overload, and +20 to the hidden Mobility stat and ×1.0625 base movement speed as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are on the low side by comparison with its peers - you can see the stats at https://destiny.report/w/3176697588/infinite-paths-8 hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/3176697588 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil is right and quite loose, so to tighten that up, look for a barrel perk that helps with Recoil Direction or, if you can handle it just go for extra handling, and then with the magazine perk go for something that helps improve magazine size or reload. For single target damage, synergising with arc builds, in the first trait perk column, Trickle Charge refills 10% of the magazine on receiving a stack of Arc Bolt Charge, and 20% when you discharge with a lightning bolt on a target, or Supercharged Magazine refills 5% of the magazine every 1.5s while you're Amplified or, after you sprint when Amplified to gain Speed Booster this accelerates to 5% every 0.25s while the buff is active, overflowing the magazine to up to ×2 capacity. Alternatively, for additional AoE damage, Dragonfly procs a 4.5m arc explosion on a crit kill - this counts as weapon damage so can inherit dmage buffs or, for your neutral game, Demolitionist generates chunk grenade ability energy on kills and an instant magazine refill on grenade use. In the second column, for damage, Voltshot applies Arc Jolt to the next target you hit within 7s (8s when enhanced), after a reload within 5.3s of a kill, for additional damage to the target on further hits plus AoE chain lightning damage on nearby enemies. The Radiolaria Transposer origin trait is useful for clearing adds - three Minors or one Major or Boss enemy kill will result in a 3m AoE explosion on death, leaving a damaging pool of radiolaria for a few seconds. The damage is treated as weapon damage so inherits damage boosts. If you're interested, "Infinite Paths," refers to the Infinite Forest Vex simulation engine on Mercury, while the "8" references Lost Prophecy, Verse 8 - the specific algorithm or riddle that Brother Vance gave us to forge the weapon back in Curse of Osiris. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Gridskipper - PvE Minor god 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling), (High-Caliber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Demoralize), (Frenzy, Aggregate Charge)
//notes:Inspired by @Saint_Kabr and @ImpetusAlways (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing groups of Minor adds in activities rated Normal difficulty, particularly with Void builds. The reprised Gridskipper is a Rapid-Fire pulse rifle which as a subfamily are currently rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ as "essentially just a worse high-impact auto". The frame intrinsic offers built-in anti-Overload, and a ×0.8 reload animation multiplier when starting a reload with an empty mag as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are pretty decent by comparison with its peers - you can see the stats at https://destiny.report/w/3176697589/gridskipper and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/3176697589 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil is vertical but loose, so to tighten that up, look for a barrel perk that helps with Recoil Direction or, if you can handle it, just go for extra Handling, and then with the magazine perk go for one of the combat effect mags or something that helps improve magazine size or reload. For Void build synergy and dealing with groups of red bar Minor adds, in the first trait perk column there's Repulsor Brace provides a ×0.5 damage resisting Void overshield for 8s (10s when enhanced) after killing a void-debuffed enemy - duration boosted by ×1.5 with Echo of Persistence, or Demoralize which spreads a ~4m AoE cloud of Void Weaken to nearby enemies for a ×1.15 boost to damage for your fireteam for 3s (5s when enhanced) on a crit kill. To pair with that in the second column, Destabilizing Rounds spreads Void Volatility on a kill in a 6.5m radius and provides Volatile Rounds for 4.5s (5.25s when enhanced). The Splicer Surge origin trait stacks up to ×3 on a reload after doing damage, offering a max +45 to Handling and Reload Speed with a ×0.9 Reload animation scalar, which lasts until stow. If you're interested, the name has a dual meaning, referring to hacking the Vex Network's digital "grid" in Season of the Splicer but also, from the lore, as a paranoid defence against Vex Wyverns "skittering" and projecting shimmering outlines in the purple "grid" of the night sky. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Gridskipper - PvE Minor god 2
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling), (High-Caliber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Demoralize), (Frenzy, Aggregate Charge)
//notes:Inspired by @Saint_Kabr and @ImpetusAlways (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing groups of Minor adds in activities rated Normal difficulty, particularly with Void builds. The reprised Gridskipper is a Rapid-Fire pulse rifle which as a subfamily are currently rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ as "essentially just a worse high-impact auto". The frame intrinsic offers built-in anti-Overload, and a ×0.8 reload animation multiplier when starting a reload with an empty mag as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are pretty decent by comparison with its peers - you can see the stats at https://destiny.report/w/3176697589/gridskipper and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/3176697589 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil is vertical but loose, so to tighten that up, look for a barrel perk that helps with Recoil Direction or, if you can handle it, just go for extra Handling, and then with the magazine perk go for one of the combat effect mags or something that helps improve magazine size or reload. For Void build synergy and dealing with groups of red bar Minor adds, in the first trait perk column there's Demoralize which spreads a ~4m AoE cloud of Void Weaken to nearby enemies for a ×1.15 boost to damage for your fireteam for 3s (5s when enhanced) on a crit kill. To pair with that in the second column, Frenzy boosts Handling and Reload Speed by +100 and boosts damage by ×1.15 for a refreshable 7.2s after being in combat for 12s - that's dealing or taking damage, or Aggregate Charge provides a stack for each different elemental debuff up to ×3 stacks to boost damage by ×1.3 (×1.33 when enhanced), with each stack lasting as long as the debuff is active - Weaken from Demoralize can constantly apply the first stack towards this. The Splicer Surge origin trait stacks up to ×3 on a reload after doing damage, offering a max +45 to Handling and Reload Speed with a ×0.9 Reload animation scalar, which lasts until stow. If you're interested, the name has a dual meaning, referring to hacking the Vex Network's digital "grid" in Season of the Splicer but also, from the lore, as a paranoid defence against Vex Wyverns "skittering" and projecting shimmering outlines in the purple "grid" of the night sky. // Gridskipper - PvE Minor god 3
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling), (High-Caliber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Demolitionist), (Desperate Measures, Aggregate Charge, Frenzy)
//notes:Inspired by @Saint_Kabr and @ImpetusAlways (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal difficulty, particularly with grenade builds. The reprised Gridskipper is a Rapid-Fire pulse rifle which as a subfamily are currently rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ as "essentially just a worse high-impact auto". The frame intrinsic offers built-in anti-Overload, and a ×0.8 reload animation multiplier when starting a reload with an empty mag as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are pretty decent by comparison with its peers - you can see the stats at https://destiny.report/w/3176697589/gridskipper and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/3176697589 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil is vertical but loose, so to tighten that up, look for a barrel perk that helps with Recoil Direction or, if you can handle it, just go for extra Handling, and then with the magazine perk go for one of the combat effect mags or something that helps improve magazine size or reload. For grenade build synergy and dealing with tougher individual adds, in the first trait perk column Demolitionist generates chunk grenade ability energy on kills and an instant magazine refill on grenade use. In the second column, you can use those grenades with Desperate Measures which provides up to ×3 stacks for ×1.3 damage for a refreshable 7s (7.5s when enhanced) for a weapon and then two ability kills or Aggregate Charge provides a stack for each different elemental debuff applied via your grenades up to ×3 stacks, to boost damage by ×1.3 (×1.33 enhanced), with each stack lasting as long as the debuff is active. Otherwise there's Frenzy which boosts Handling and Reload Speed by +100 and damage by ×1.15 for a refreshable 7.2s after being in combat for 12s - that's dealing or taking damage. The Splicer Surge origin trait stacks up to ×3 on a reload after doing damage, offering a max +45 to Handling and Reload Speed with a ×0.9 Reload animation scalar, which lasts until stow. If you're interested, the name has a dual meaning, referring to hacking the Vex Network's digital "grid" in Season of the Splicer but also, from the lore, as a paranoid defence against Vex Wyverns "skittering" and projecting shimmering outlines in the purple "grid" of the night sky. // Mint Retrograde - PvE Majors god 1
// (Quick Launch, Smart Drift Control, Hard Launch), (Tactical Mag, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Rewind Rounds), (Frenzy, Cascade Point)
//notes:Inspired by @Saint_Kabr and @ImpetusAlways (PvE PvE-Boss PvE-BurstDamage PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for burst DPS against a single orange or yellow-bar target, such as a Bane, Elite, Champion or Miniboss in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Mint Retrograde is a Micro-Missile pulse rifle which as a subfamily which as a subfamily are currently rated S-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ providing "game-warping levels of add clear, nearly 2x damage per brick vs. special ammo HC, forgiving crit multi". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Unstoppable and micro-missile projectiles that track locked-on targets as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are decent by comparison with its peers - you can see them at https://destiny.report/w/3285784871/mint-retrograde and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/3285784871 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. Look for a barrel perk that improves Handling, and then with the magazine perk go for something that helps improve magazine size or reload. For burst damage against beefier enemies, in the first trait perk column Rewind Rounds refills 60% of hits landed (70% when enhanced) from reserves when the magazine empties. For burst damage, you need a second column trait perk that doesn't require too much prep - Frenzy, after being in combat for 12s - that's dealing or taking damage - boosts Handling and Reload Speed by +100 and boosts damage by ×1.15 for a refreshable 7.2s or, if you're dealing with Majors in add groups, Cascade Point builds up on crit hits with other weapons and provides "Cascade Point Ready" for 5s when procced - readying this weapon grants Cascade Point reducing firing delay to ×0.6 for 2.5s (2.75s when enhanced) to boost DPS, if not total damage. If you're lucky you'll drop one with the Accelerated Assault origin trait which passively refills 10% of the magazine, and boosts damage by ×1.05 for getting 3 hits within a 1.6s window, but the Problem Solver Origin Trait is also very good for PvE when facing orange and yellow bars - 11 hits (which, from Mint Retrograde, can be either impact or explosive damage, so two 3-round bursts is 12 hits) will proc to exhaust them and reduce damage output to ×0.75 for 5s and grant +50 to Reload Speed and Handling for 10s. There's also Vanguard Vindication which provides up to ×3 stacks that reduces incoming damage to max ×0.94 for a refreshable 10s (12.5s when enhanced), on kills within 20s of using a class ability. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Boss PvE-BurstDamage PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Mint Retrograde - PvE Majors god 2
// (Quick Launch, Smart Drift Control, Hard Launch), (Tactical Mag, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Field Prep), (Frenzy, Elemental Honing)
//notes:Inspired by @Saint_Kabr and @ImpetusAlways (PvE PvE-Boss PvE-BurstDamage PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for burst DPS against a single orange or yellow-bar target, such as a Bane, Elite, Champion or Miniboss in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Mint Retrograde is a Micro-Missile pulse rifle which as a subfamily which as a subfamily are currently rated S-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ providing "game-warping levels of add clear, nearly 2x damage per brick vs. special ammo HC, forgiving crit multi". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Unstoppable and micro-missile projectiles that track locked-on targets as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are decent by comparison with its peers - you can see them at https://destiny.report/w/3285784871/mint-retrograde and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/3285784871 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. Look for a barrel perk that improves Handling, and then with the magazine perk go for something that helps improve magazine size or reload. For burst damage against beefier enemies, in the first trait perk column Field Prep bumps the very low Ammo Generation stat by +30 and grants +50 Reload Speed with a ×0.8 animation scalar to both reload and ready/stow when crouched (+40 Ammo Generation and +55 Reload Speed when enhanced) or Hatchling spawns a Strand Threadling on a crit kill or three crit hits within 3s of each other for additional damage. For burst damage, you need a second column trait perk that doesn't require too much prep - Frenzy, after being in combat for 12s - that's dealing or taking damage - boosts Handling and Reload Speed by +100 and boosts damage by ×1.15 for a refreshable 7.2s or, for Prismatic builds, Elemental Honing grants an escalating stack of damage up to ×1.35 for 20s (23s when enhanced) for each elemental damage type inflicted, which can be refreshed with a new damage type until you've run through all 5. If you're lucky you'll drop one with the Accelerated Assault origin trait which passively refills 10% of the magazine, and boosts damage by ×1.05 for getting 3 hits within a 1.6s window, but the Problem Solver Origin Trait is also very good for PvE when facing orange and yellow bars - 11 hits (which, from Mint Retrograde, can be either impact or explosive damage, so two 3-round bursts is 12 hits) will proc to exhaust them and reduce damage output to ×0.75 for 5s and grant +50 to Reload Speed and Handling for 10s. There's also Vanguard Vindication which provides up to ×3 stacks that reduces incoming damage to max ×0.94 for a refreshable 10s (12.5s when enhanced), on kills within 20s of using a class ability. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Boss PvE-BurstDamage PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

For damage in the second column, Bait and Switch boosts damage by ×1.3 for 10s (11s when enhanced) for doing damage with your other two weapons, or Elemental Honing grants an escalating stack of damage up to ×1.35 max for 20s (23s when enhanced) for each elemental damage type inflicted, which can be refreshed with a new damage type until you've run through all 5. // Mint Retrograde - PvE Majors god 2
// (Quick Launch, Smart Drift Control, Hard Launch), (Tactical Mag, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Field Prep), (Frenzy, Elemental Honing)
//notes:Inspired by @Saint_Kabr and @ImpetusAlways (PvE PvE-Boss PvE-BurstDamage PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for burst DPS against a single orange or yellow-bar target, such as a Bane, Elite, Champion or Miniboss in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Mint Retrograde is a Micro-Missile pulse rifle which as a subfamily which as a subfamily are currently rated S-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ providing "game-warping levels of add clear, nearly 2x damage per brick vs. special ammo HC, forgiving crit multi". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Unstoppable and micro-missile projectiles that track locked-on targets as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are decent by comparison with its peers - you can see them at https://destiny.report/w/1715391576/mint-retrograde and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/1715391576 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. Look for a barrel perk that improves Handling, and then with the magazine perk go for something that helps improve magazine size or reload. For burst damage against beefier enemies, in the first trait perk column Field Prep bumps the very low Ammo Generation stat by +30 and grants +50 Reload Speed with a ×0.8 animation scalar to both reload and ready/stow when crouched (+40 Ammo Generation and +55 Reload Speed when enhanced) or Hatchling spawns a Strand Threadling on a crit kill or three crit hits within 3s of each other for additional damage. For burst damage, you need a second column trait perk that doesn't require too much prep - Frenzy, after being in combat for 12s - that's dealing or taking damage - boosts Handling and Reload Speed by +100 and boosts damage by ×1.15 for a refreshable 7.2s or, for Prismatic builds, Elemental Honing grants an escalating stack of damage up to ×1.35 for 20s (23s when enhanced) for each elemental damage type inflicted, which can be refreshed with a new damage type until you've run through all 5. If you're lucky you'll drop one with the Accelerated Assault origin trait which passively refills 10% of the magazine, and boosts damage by ×1.05 for getting 3 hits within a 1.6s window, but the Problem Solver Origin Trait is also very good for PvE when facing orange and yellow bars - 11 hits (which, from Mint Retrograde, can be either impact or explosive damage, so two 3-round bursts is 12 hits) will proc to exhaust them and reduce damage output to ×0.75 for 5s and grant +50 to Reload Speed and Handling for 10s. There's also Vanguard Vindication which provides up to ×3 stacks that reduces incoming damage to max ×0.94 for a refreshable 10s (12.5s when enhanced), on kills within 20s of using a class ability. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Boss PvE-BurstDamage PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Insidious - PvE Minor god 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Polygonal Rifling, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling), (Tactical Mag, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Frenzy), (Chaos Reshaped, One For All, Rampage)
//notes:Inspired by @Saint_Kabr and @ImpetusAlways (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Very Hard, Hard or Normal difficulty. The updated Insidious is an Aggresive Burst pulse rifle which as a subfamily are currently rated D-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ as "essentially just a poor high-impact auto, but arguably worse due to 4-burst nature". The frame intrinsic offers built-in anti-Unstoppable, and fires a 4-round burst as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are pretty much best in class by comparison with its peers - you can see the stats at https://destiny.report/w/3428521585/insidious and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/3428521585 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil is right and a little loose, so to tighten that up, look for a barrel perk that helps with Recoil Direction or, if you can handle it just go for extra stability (you'll get handling from the trait perks), and then with the magazine perk go for something that helps improve magazine size, stability or reload. For single target damage, while there are some decent first trait perk column perks, it's difficult to look beyond Frenzy which, after being in combat for 12s - that's dealing or taking damage - boosts Handling and Reload Speed by +100 and boosts damage by ×1.15 for a refreshable 7.2s. In the second column, for even more multiplicative damage, Chaos Reshaped stacks up to ×2 on dealing or receiving damage within a 5s window for at least 12s (11.5s when enhanced) - at ×1 it provides a ×1.2 damage boost for a refreshable 7s and then at ×2 provides ×1.35 damage for a refreshable 7s together with a healing burst of up to 35HP that also starts health regeneration. There's also One For All which boosts damage by ×1.35 for a non-refreshable 10s (11s when enhanced) after hitting three separate targets within 3s of each and Rampage which stacks up to ×3 on kills for a max ×1.331 damage for a refreshable 4.5s (5s when enhanced), with the stacks decaying one at a time. The Souldrinker origin trait is very good for endgame activities, restoring up to +50HP on reload, depending on the proportion of the magazine landed as hits. If you're interested, "insidious" means proceeding in a gradual, subtle way, but with harmful effects, reflecting the modus operandi of The Witness and its Disciple, Rhulk. // Insidious - PvE Minor god 2
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Polygonal Rifling, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling), (Tactical Mag, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Frenzy), (Voltshot)
//notes:Inspired by @Saint_Kabr and @ImpetusAlways (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing groups of Minor adds in activities rated Very Hard, Hard or Normal difficulty, particularly with Arc builds. The updated Insidious is an Aggressive Burst pulse rifle which as a subfamily are currently rated D-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ as "essentially just a poor high-impact auto, but arguably worse due to 4-burst nature". The frame intrinsic offers built-in anti-Unstoppable, and fires a 4-round burst as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are pretty much best in class by comparison with its peers - you can see the stats at https://destiny.report/w/3428521585/insidious and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/3428521585 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil is right and a little loose, so to tighten that up, look for a barrel perk that helps with Recoil Direction or, if you can handle it just go for extra stability (you'll get handling from the trait perks), and then with the magazine perk go for something that helps improve magazine size, stability or reload. For single target damage, while there are some decent first trait perk column perks, it's difficult to look beyond Frenzy which, after being in combat for 12s - that's dealing or taking damage - boosts Handling and Reload Speed by +100 and boosts damage by ×1.15 for a refreshable 7.2s. In the second column, for arc synergy and add groups, Voltshot applies Arc Jolt to the next target you hit within 7s (8s when enhanced), after a reload within 5.3s of a kill, for additional damage to the target on further hits plus AoE chain lightning damage on nearby enemies. The Souldrinker origin trait is very good for endgame activities, restoring up to +50HP on reload, depending on the proportion of the magazine landed as hits. If you're interested, "insidious" means proceeding in a gradual, subtle way, but with harmful effects, reflecting the modus operandi of The Witness and its Disciple, Rhulk. Recommended MW: Stability or Range, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Insidious (Adept) - PvE Minor god 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Polygonal Rifling, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling), (Tactical Mag, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Frenzy), (Chaos Reshaped, One For All, Rampage)
//notes:Inspired by @Saint_Kabr and @ImpetusAlways (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Very Hard, Hard or Normal difficulty. The updated Insidious is an Aggresive Burst pulse rifle which as a subfamily are currently rated D-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ as "essentially just a poor high-impact auto, but arguably worse due to 4-burst nature". The frame intrinsic offers built-in anti-Unstoppable, and fires a 4-round burst as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are pretty much best in class by comparison with its peers - you can see the stats at https://destiny.report/w/786352912/insidious and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/786352912 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil is right and a little loose, so to tighten that up, look for a barrel perk that helps with Recoil Direction or, if you can handle it just go for extra stability (you'll get handling from the trait perks), and then with the magazine perk go for something that helps improve magazine size, stability or reload. For single target damage, while there are some decent first trait perk column perks, it's difficult to look beyond Frenzy which, after being in combat for 12s - that's dealing or taking damage - boosts Handling and Reload Speed by +100 and boosts damage by ×1.15 for a refreshable 7.2s. In the second column, for even more multiplicative damage, Chaos Reshaped stacks up to ×2 on dealing or receiving damage within a 5s window for at least 12s (11.5s when enhanced) - at ×1 it provides a ×1.2 damage boost for a refreshable 7s and then at ×2 provides ×1.35 damage for a refreshable 7s together with a healing burst of up to 35HP that also starts health regeneration. There's also One For All which boosts damage by ×1.35 for a non-refreshable 10s (11s when enhanced) after hitting three separate targets within 3s of each and Rampage which stacks up to ×3 on kills for a max ×1.331 damage for a refreshable 4.5s (5s when enhanced), with the stacks decaying one at a time. The Souldrinker origin trait is very good for endgame activities, restoring up to +50HP on reload, depending on the proportion of the magazine landed as hits. If you're interested, "insidious" means proceeding in a gradual, subtle way, but with harmful effects, reflecting the modus operandi of The Witness and its Disciple, Rhulk. Recommended MW: Stability or Range, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Insidious (Adept) - PvE Minor god 2
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Polygonal Rifling, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling), (Tactical Mag, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Frenzy), (Voltshot)
//notes:Inspired by @Saint_Kabr and @ImpetusAlways (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing groups of Minor adds in activities rated Very Hard, Hard or Normal difficulty, particularly with Arc builds. The updated Insidious is an Aggressive Burst pulse rifle which as a subfamily are currently rated D-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ as "essentially just a poor high-impact auto, but arguably worse due to 4-burst nature". The frame intrinsic offers built-in anti-Unstoppable, and fires a 4-round burst as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are pretty much best in class by comparison with its peers - you can see the stats at https://destiny.report/w/786352912/insidious and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/786352912 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil is right and a little loose, so to tighten that up, look for a barrel perk that helps with Recoil Direction or, if you can handle it just go for extra stability (you'll get handling from the trait perks), and then with the magazine perk go for something that helps improve magazine size, stability or reload. For single target damage, while there are some decent first trait perk column perks, it's difficult to look beyond Frenzy which, after being in combat for 12s - that's dealing or taking damage - boosts Handling and Reload Speed by +100 and boosts damage by ×1.15 for a refreshable 7.2s. In the second column, for arc synergy and add groups, Voltshot applies Arc Jolt to the next target you hit within 7s (8s when enhanced), after a reload within 5.3s of a kill, for additional damage to the target on further hits plus AoE chain lightning damage on nearby enemies. The Souldrinker origin trait is very good for endgame activities, restoring up to +50HP on reload, depending on the proportion of the magazine landed as hits. If you're interested, "insidious" means proceeding in a gradual, subtle way, but with harmful effects, reflecting the modus operandi of The Witness and its Disciple, Rhulk. Recommended MW: Stability or Range, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// The Third Axiom - PvE Utility god 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling), (Armor-Piercing Rounds, High-Caliber Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Appended Mag, Alloy Mag, Flared Magwell), (Genesis, Slideways), (Disruption Break)
//notes:Inspired by @Saint_Kabr and @ImpetusAlways (PvE PvE-Utility PvE-Champion PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for piercing Barrier Champions in activities rated Very Hard, Hard or Normal difficulty. The Third Axiom is an Adaptive pulse rifle which as a subfamily are currently rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ as "small step down from C tier pulse damage profile, but has barrier intrinsic". The frame intrinsic offers no bonuses beyond being "well-rounded, reliable and sturdy", as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are on the low side by comparison with its peers - you can see the stats at https://destiny.report/w/3744058588/the-third-axiom and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/3744058588 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil is a little left and a bit loose, so to tighten that up, look for a barrel perk that helps with Recoil Direction or, if you can handle it just go for extra handling, and then with the magazine perk go for Armor-Piercing Rounds for the additional ×1.05 shield damage, one of the other cambat effect mags or something that helps improve magazine size or reload. This is, admittedly, a niche roll but if you pair Genesis in the first trait perk column, to refill the mag after breaking a shield or Slideways to refill 15% of the mag and boost Stability by +15 and Handling by +30 (+18 and +33 when enhanced) when you slide, with Disruption Break in the second column to increase kinetic damage by ×1.5 for 5.5s after breaking an elemental shield or barrier, you should be able to quickly finish off Barrier Champions from a safe distance with a kinetic special weapon. The Vanguard Determination origin trait provides up to ×3 stacks that reduces incoming damage to max ×0.94 for a refreshable 10s (12.5s when enhanced), on kills within 20s of using a class ability. If you're interested, in mathematics, the "Third Axiom of Probability" (the axiom of countable additivity) dictates how separate, mutually exclusive events function - this ties perfectly into the weapon's flavour text. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Utility PvE-Champion PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Hailing Confusion - PvE Minor god 1
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling), (High-Caliber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Alloy Mag, Appended Mag, Flared Magwell), (Rimestealer, Headstone), (Crystalline Corpsebloom)
//notes:Inspired by @Saint_Kabr and @ImpetusAlways (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing groups of Minor adds in activities rated Normal difficulty, particularly with Stasis builds. Hailing Confusion returns as an Adaptive pulse rifle which as a subfamily are currently rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ as "small step down from C tier pulse damage profile, but has barrier intrinsic". The frame intrinsic offers no bonuses beyond "being well-rounded and reliable" as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are decent by comparison with its peers - you can see them at https://destiny.report/w/4236134153/hailing-confusion and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/4236134153 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil is left and loose, so to tighten that up, look for a barrel perk that helps with Recoil Direction or, if you can handle it just go for extra handling, and then with the magazine perk go for any of the combat effect mags or something that helps improve magazine size or reload. For Stasis synergy and clearing groups of adds in general content, in the first trait perk column Rimestealer generates ×1 stack (×2 stacks when enhanced) of Stasis Frost Armor on shattering a Stasis crystal or killing a frozen enemy, or Headstone generates a Stasis crystal on a crit kill. In the second column, Crystalline Corpsebloom procs on a kill to provide a 4s buff - kills with the buff active generate a Stasis seeker which travels 3m before spawning a medium Stasis crystal (large when enhanced). The Stasis crystals are weapon-generated entities so can inherit damage buffs, and proc Stasis subclass fragments when shattered for AoE damage. The Winterized Gear origin trait provides ×3 stacks after 12s out of combat - each stack decays after 5s in combat providing a gradually reducing boost to Stability, Handling and Reload Speed. If you're interested, the name reflect the harsh, disorienting nature of Europa's freezing storms as well as Clovis Bray's calculated military philosophy. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Hailing Confusion - PvE Minor god 2
// (Arrowhead Brake, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Fluted Barrel, Corkscrew Rifling), (High-Caliber Rounds, Armor-Piercing Rounds, Ricochet Rounds, Alloy Mag, Appended Mag, Flared Magwell), (Demolitionist, Headstone), (Meganeura)
//notes:Inspired by @Saint_Kabr and @ImpetusAlways (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing groups of Minor adds in activities rated Normal difficulty, particularly with Stasis builds. Hailing Confusion returns as an Adaptive pulse rifle which as a subfamily are currently rated C-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis spreadsheet in https://www.destiny2.science/ as "small step down from C tier pulse damage profile, but has barrier intrinsic". The frame intrinsic offers no bonuses beyond "being well-rounded and reliable" as per the https://www.destiny2.science/ Destiny Data Compendium. The stats are decent by comparison with its peers - you can see them at https://destiny.report/w/4236134153/hailing-confusion and hopefully https://d2foundry.gg/w/4236134153 will show how weapon stats impact in-game performance when the site is back up and running. The recoil is left and loose, so to tighten that up, look for a barrel perk that helps with Recoil Direction or, if you can handle it just go for extra handling, and then with the magazine perk go for any of the combat effect mags or something that helps improve magazine size or reload. For Stasis synergy and clearing groups of adds in general content, in the first trait perk column Headstone generates a Stasis crystal on a crit kill - these are weapon-generated entities so can inherit damage buffs, and proc Stasis subclass fragments when shattered for AoE damage. In the second column, Meganeura builds up to ×3 stacks on crit hits for a refreshable 3.5s (4s when enhanced) for AoE explosive damage in a 7.5m radius on a crit kill. Unfortunately, the explosion occurs slightly before the Headstone stasis crystal is formed, so you'll need to shatter the crystal manually. The Winterized Gear origin trait provides ×3 stacks after 12s out of combat - each stack decays after 5s in combat providing a gradually reducing boost to Stability, Handling and Reload Speed. If you're interested, the name reflect the harsh, disorienting nature of Europa's freezing storms as well as Clovis Bray's calculated military philosophy. Recommended MW: Handling, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-God M+KB Controller
